## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [1] [Object-centric LeJEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02404)
*Jakob Geusen, Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Object-centric LeJEPA，通过利用 SAM 提供的物体掩码，将 LeJEPA 的联合嵌入预测架构从整图对齐扩展到物体级对齐，在跟踪、分类、分割和重识别任务上优于图像级 LeJEPA，且数据效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像级自监督学习方法（如 LeJEPA）通常需要大量训练数据，而物体级表征对齐有望提升数据效率。然而，完全自监督地同时进行场景分割和物体表征存在循环依赖的不稳定性。本文旨在绕过这一困难，利用现成的 SAM 掩码实现稳定的物体级表征学习。

Method: 将 LeJEPA 的分布性抗坍缩目标从整图扩展到可变大小的物体集合，利用 SAM 提供的物体掩码作为训练时的先验。此外，引入实例分离损失，将同一场景中的其他物体作为负样本，进一步提升下游性能。实验在两种模型规模和 COCO 的 10%-100% 数据上进行。

Result: 在 DAVIS（跟踪）、ImageNet-1k（分类）、ADE20k（分割）和 NAVI（重识别）上，物体级 LeJEPA 一致优于图像级 LeJEPA，且数据效率更高。

Conclusion: 利用现成掩码进行物体级连续表征对齐是一种有效且数据高效的自监督学习方法，为未来更细粒度的视觉表征学习提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文扩展 LeJEPA（一种基于联合嵌入预测架构的连续表征学习方法）到物体级别，通过 SAM 掩码对齐物体级连续视觉表征，属于连续视觉 tokenizer 与表征自编码器方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous visual tokenizer and representation autoencoder subtopic with object-level alignment, boosting data efficiency and downstream performance. Signals: Object-centric LeJEPA, SAM masks for object-level alignment, data efficiency, outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking, classification, segmentation, re-identification, extends joint embedding predictive architecture

Abstract: Image encoders trained with LeJEPA can deliver strong features for downstream tasks, but, like other image-level self-supervised methods, typically require large training datasets. Aligning representations at the level of objects rather than whole scenes promises greater data efficiency, but doing this in a completely self-supervised way, effectively jointly partitioning a scene and representing its objects, is unstable: the two are locked in a cyclic dependency, partitioning requires meaningful representations, while meaningful representations require consistent partitioning. We sidestep this instability by taking object masks as given during training, using cheap, off-the-shelf SAM proposals. We extend LeJEPA - whose distributional anti-collapse objective ports naturally from whole images to variable-sized sets of objects - to align object-centric representations rather than whole images. An additional instance-separating loss, which treats other objects in the same scene as negatives, further boosts downstream performance. Across two model scales and 10-100% of COCO, object-level LeJEPA outperforms image-level LeJEPA on tracking (DAVIS), classification (ImageNet-1k), segmentation (ADE20k), and re-identification (NAVI).

</details>

### [2] [Subliminal Clocks: Latent Time Modelling in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01774)
*Maximo Rulli, Thomas Fontanari, Simone Petruzzi, Federico Alvetreti, Giorgio Strano, Donato Crisostomi, Giorgos Nikolaou, Tommaso Mencattini, Andrea Santilli, Emanuele Rodolà, Simone Scardapane, Alessio Devoto*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散语言模型（DLM）在残差流中隐式编码了与扩散时间步相关的潜在表征，可通过探针可靠提取，并能在低维子空间上引导模型以系统性地调节去噪进度、置信度和熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）不像标准扩散模型那样显式以时间步为条件，因此自然产生一个问题：DLM 是否在内部表征去噪进度？这种信息如何被下游使用？

Method: 使用线性探针从 DLM 各层残差流中解码与扩散时间步相关的信号；通过沿推断时间步对应的低维子空间进行引导（steering），调节模型的去噪进度感知；分析该表征在激活空间中的几何结构。

Result: 探针能在各层可靠提取时间步信号；沿低维子空间引导可系统性地改变模型置信度和熵；该表征在激活空间中具有结构化和可解释的几何性质。

Conclusion: DLM 内部确实编码了去噪进度的潜在表征，且该表征可被提取和操控，为理解扩散语言模型的内部工作机制提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）内部对去噪时间步的隐式表征，属于连续潜空间中的语言建模，与 [CANONICAL] 连续语言模型与语言流 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language model understanding by revealing latent timestep encoding and steerability in diffusion language models. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, latent representation, interpretability, steering

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Unlike standard diffusion-based approaches, DLMs are not explicitly conditioned on a timestep, raising a natural question: do these models internally represent denoising progress, and how is such information used downstream? In this work, we show that DLMs do in fact encode a latent representation related to the diffusion timestep within their residual streams. We find that this signal can be reliably extracted using probes across layers, indicating that denoising progress is decodable from internal activations. We further demonstrate that steering the model along a low-dimensional subspace associated with the inferred timestep allows us to systematically modulate its notion of denoising progress, leading to predictable changes in model confidence and entropy. Finally, we analyse the geometry of the identified representation, showing that it exhibits structured and interpretable properties in activation space, and shedding light on how such a signal is processed by these models.

</details>

### [3] [Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models via Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01378)
*William English, Hao Zheng, Rickard Ewetz*

Main category: eess.SY, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号安全引导机制，用于基于流匹配的VLA模型。该方法将安全约束形式化为最小范数约束优化问题，在去噪过程中修正轨迹预测中的安全违规，实现预测性碰撞避免而非被动反应。在SafeLIBERO基准上，碰撞避免率达82.8%，任务成功率81.6%，分别比单步方法提升6.3%和19.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的安全措施仅能防止机器人下一步动作导致的碰撞，缺乏对长时序轨迹的预测性安全约束。随着任务时域增长，分布偏移加剧，单步安全方法难以应对累积风险。

Method: 将安全约束形式化为最小范数约束优化问题，在流匹配去噪过程中对中间轨迹预测进行修正。通过分析预测轨迹并在迭代去噪中施加符号约束，实现预测性碰撞避免，将符号约束满足与神经轨迹生成交织在一起。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO基准上，碰撞避免率82.8%，任务成功率81.6%，分别比单步方法提升6.3%和19.8%。在长时域任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全引导机制能有效提升VLA模型在机器人操作中的安全性，特别是在长时域任务中，预测性碰撞避免优于传统的反应式安全干预。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的神经符号安全引导机制，通过约束流匹配实现机器人操作中的预测性碰撞避免。核心是具身智能场景下的动作规划与安全控制，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Integrates safety constraints into flow matching for VLA models, relevant to world models and continuous multimodal reasoning but not directly on priority subtopics. Signals: flow matching, safety guidance, VLA models, world model, constrained optimization

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their real-world deployment remains limited by the lack of effective safety measures. Specifically, existing safety measures only prevent collisions caused by the robot's next action. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic safety guidance mechanism for flow matching based VLAs that enables predictive collision avoidance. Flow matching based VLAs determine the next actions by predicting a trajectory (a sequence of actions) through an iterative neural flow matching process. Our method formulates safety enforcement as a minimum-norm constrained optimization problem that corrects safety violations during the denoising process of noisy intermediate trajectory predictions. By analyzing predicted trajectories and applying corrections during iterative denoising, our approach anticipates collisions before they become unavoidable. This interleaving of symbolic constraint satisfaction with neural trajectory generation enables predictive collision avoidance rather than reactive intervention. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, our method achieves 82.8% collision avoidance and 81.6% task success, a 6.3% and 19.8% improvement respectively over single-step methods, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks where compounding distribution shift is most pronounced. Video demonstrations of our approach are included on our project page at .

</details>


### [4] [BIFROST: Bridging Invariant Feature Representation for Observation-space Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01410)
*Yunfu Deng, Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (63.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BIFROST 提出通过跨域双模拟目标学习共享历史编码器，从原始观测中提取跨仿真与真实环境的共享结构，使在仿真中训练的 policy 能零样本迁移到真实机器人任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sim2real 迁移面临仿真与真实环境在渲染和物理上的双重差异，现有方法通常分别处理每种差异并组合适配模块，但忽略了仿真与真实任务之间存在的共享结构。本文旨在从原始观测中识别并利用这种共享结构，实现零样本迁移。

Method: 提出 BIFROST 框架，在配对的跨域数据上通过跨域双模拟（cross-domain bisimulation）目标学习共享历史编码器：将导致等价长期行为的观测-动作序列映射到相近的潜状态，忽略领域差异。策略在仿真环境的潜状态上训练，直接零样本迁移到真实环境。

Result: 在 sim2sim 视觉导航、sim2real 接触丰富的操作任务和视觉伺服任务上，BIFROST 实现了有效的迁移，而领域自适应和协同训练基线在视觉和动力学双重域差异下均失败。

Conclusion: BIFROST 证明了通过跨域双模拟目标学习共享潜表征，可以有效弥合仿真与真实环境之间的视觉和动力学差异，实现机器人策略的零样本 sim2real 迁移。

Classification reason: 该论文研究机器人策略学习的 sim2real 迁移，通过跨域双模拟目标学习共享历史编码器，使仿真训练的 policy 零样本迁移到真实环境。这属于具身智能中世界模型与策略规划控制的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong sim2real transfer method for robotics, but only tangentially touches user's priority on continuous multimodal models and world models. Signals: sim2real transfer, cross-domain bisimulation, zero-shot policy transfer, visual navigation, contact-rich manipulation

Abstract: Sim2real transfer for robot policy learning suffers due to mismatch between simulation and reality. Existing methods typically address each gap in isolation through separate adaptation modules, which are composed or layered when both gaps coexist. Yet the basis for attempting sim2real in the first place is that there is shared structure between a task in simulation and reality, where equivalent actions from equivalent configurations produce equivalent long term outcomes regardless of domain specific differences in rendering or physics. In this paper, we study whether we can identify and exploit this shared structure from raw observations to train a policy that enables zero shot transfer. We introduce BIFROST, which learns a shared history encoder on paired cross-domain data via cross-domain bisimulation objective: observation-action sequences leading to equivalent long-term behavior are mapped to nearby latent states, regardless of domain. Policies trained on these latent states in simulation transfer zero-shot to reality. We provide empirical evidence on sim2sim visual navigation and sim2real contact rich manipulation task and visual servoing task that BIFROST achieves effective transfer where domain adaptation and co-training baselines fail under both visual and dynamics domain gaps.

</details>


### [5] [CoRe: Combined Rewards with Vision-Language Model Feedback for Preference-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01721)
*Hexian Ni, Tao Lu, Yinghao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (56.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoRe提出了一种结合形式化奖励（FR）和残差奖励（RR）的混合框架，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）反馈自动构建偏好对齐的奖励信号，无需人工参与，在10个仿真和5个真实机器人操作任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的奖励设计一直是一个核心挑战：手工设计的奖励难以指定且可能导致次优策略，而从偏好中学习的奖励则存在效率低和训练不稳定的问题。受认知科学中人类学习的双重性质启发，作者希望将奖励分解为形式化奖励和残差奖励两部分，并利用VLM自动构建偏好对齐的奖励。

Method: CoRe包含两个模块：(1) 形式化奖励模块（FRM），利用VLM根据任务知识和偏好反馈迭代设计和优化形式化奖励；(2) 残差奖励模块（RRM），利用VLM生成视频级别的偏好标签，学习捕捉隐式偏好的残差奖励，补充形式化奖励的不足。两者协同实现无需人工参与的偏好对齐策略学习。

Result: 在10个仿真机器人操作任务和5个真实世界任务上的实验表明，CoRe在策略学习效果和效率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CoRe通过形式化奖励与残差奖励的协同，结合VLM反馈，实现了高效且偏好对齐的自动奖励构建，为机器人操作任务中的奖励设计提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CoRe框架，利用VLM反馈设计形式化奖励和残差奖励，用于机器人操作任务的强化学习策略对齐，属于具身智能中的奖励设计与策略学习，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics RL reward design but low alignment with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: VLM feedback for reward, robotic manipulation, preference alignment, formal+residual rewards, no continuous token or video generation focus

Abstract: Reward design remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL). Hand-crafted rewards are often difficult to specify and may lead to suboptimal policies, while learned rewards from preferences can suffer from inefficiency and unstable training. Inspired by the dual nature of human learning explored in cognitive science, we decompose rewards into two complementary components: Formal Rewards (FR), explicitly designed based on task knowledge, and Residual Rewards (RR), learned from observations to capture implicit and nuanced preferences. Based on this decomposition, we propose CoRe, a hybrid framework that integrates FR and RR with vision-language models (VLMs) feedback to achieve preference-aligned policies without human involvement. Our contributions are twofold: (1) We propose a Formal Reward Module (FRM) that leverages VLMs to iteratively design and optimize FR based on task knowledge and preference feedback, enabling the continual improvement of policy during training; (2) We introduce a Residual Reward Module (RRM) that learns RR from video-level preference by employing VLMs to generate preference labels and capturing nuanced rewards that complement FR, ensuring alignment with human intent. Through the synergy of FRM and RRM, CoRe enables the automatic construction of reliable rewards that are efficient and preference-aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoRe outperforms existing approaches in terms of policy learning effectiveness and efficiency on ten robotic manipulation tasks in simulation and five real-worlds. Videos can be found on our project website:

</details>


### [6] [Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092)
*Liuhaichen Yang, Zhuang Jiang, Chenchao Sheng, Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (64.34) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Guided Action Flow 提出了一种推理时引导框架，在保持预训练 SmolVLA 策略冻结的情况下，使用从真实成功/失败轨迹中学习的动作块评论家（critic）来引导 flow-matching 反向采样过程，在 LIBERO 操作任务上提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-matching VLA 策略通过迭代传输过程生成机器人动作块，这为测试时引导提供了机会，而无需重新训练基础策略。现有方法通常需要重新训练或微调策略本身，作者希望探索能否通过推理时引导来提升冻结策略的性能。

Method: 提出 Guided Action Flow 框架：保持预训练的 SmolVLA 策略冻结，训练一个动作块评论家（critic），该评论家从真实成功/失败轨迹中学习，可条件于 SmolVLA 语言路径的任务描述特征，在采样过程中仅通过动作梯度引导反向 flow 采样器。

Result: 在 LIBERO 操作任务上，单任务评论家将成功率从 68.0% 提升至 82.0%（一个种子窗口）和从 82.0% 提升至 86.0%（另一个种子窗口）。多任务描述评论家将验证成功率从 46.0% 提升至 56.0%，但锁定的保留测试集增益较小（65.0% 到 67.5%）。

Conclusion: 实验支持了 Q-guided 推理对冻结的 flow-matching VLA 策略的可行性，同时表明评论家泛化能力和不确定性感知引导仍是核心瓶颈。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Guided Action Flow，使用 flow-matching 的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略生成机器人动作块，并通过 Q-guided 推理在测试时引导采样，属于具身智能中的机器人动作规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Directly applies flow-matching and critic-guided inference to robot action, aligning with world_model and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: flow-matching guidance, frozen VLA policy, action-chunk critic, LIBERO manipulation, inference-time intervention

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action policies generate robot action chunks through an iterative transport process, creating an opportunity for test-time guidance without retraining the base policy. We study this opportunity in Guided Action Flow, an inference-time framework that keeps a pretrained SmolVLA policy frozen and uses a learned action-chunk critic to guide its reverse-time flow sampler. The critic is trained from real success and failure rollouts, can condition on task-description features from the frozen SmolVLA language pathway, and is used only through action gradients during sampling. We evaluate the approach on LIBERO manipulation tasks. A single-task critic improves success from 68.0% to 82.0% on one seed window and from 82.0% to 86.0% on another. A multi-family task-description critic improves validation success from 46.0% to 56.0%, while the locked held-out test gain is positive but modest, from 65.0% to 67.5%. These results support the feasibility of Q-guided inference for frozen flow-matching VLA policies, while showing that critic generalization and uncertainty-aware guidance remain the central bottlenecks.

</details>

*Guoyang Xia, Fengfa Li, Hongjin Ji, Lei Ren, Fangxiang Feng, Kun Zhan, Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAFlow 提出了一个统一的 flow-matching 框架，用于对比不同 VLA 预训练范式（纯动作建模、语言监督共训练、未来潜空间对齐及其组合），在异构机器人数据集 OXEMix 上训练，实验表明语言监督与未来潜空间对齐的联合训练（MindLWPI）能最稳定地迁移到多个操作基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在架构、数据、动作空间和评估协议上差异巨大，难以公平比较不同预训练范式对下游操作任务的影响。作者希望建立一个统一的框架来系统对比不同训练目标的效果。

Method: 构建异构机器人数据集 OXEMix（约5000小时数据），采用统一的 pi0 风格架构（共享 VLM 主干 + 动作专家），固定14维动作空间，对比四种范式：MindPI（纯动作建模）、MindLPI（语言监督共训练）、MindWPI（未来潜空间对齐）、MindLWPI（三者组合）。使用 flow matching 进行动作生成。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 SimplerEnv 上，纯动作预训练对异构数据敏感；语言监督有助于保持视觉-语言泛化能力；未来潜空间对齐改善状态转移与动作结果建模；MindLWPI（三者组合）在多个基准上取得最稳定的迁移性能。

Conclusion: 语言监督和未来潜空间表征提供了互补的中间约束，使异构动作监督更平滑、更易迁移，支持"元动作空间"的观点。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VLAFlow，一个面向视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的统一训练框架，涉及机器人操作、动作条件建模与未来潜空间对齐，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，因此映射到 world_model 方向下的 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a unified flow-matching framework for VLA pre-training, directly relevant to continuous multimodal models and world models, with strong empirical validation. Signals: flow-matching framework, vision-language-action models, future latent alignment, heterogeneous robot data, world model

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have recently advanced robotic manipulation, yet the effects of different robot-data pre-training paradigms remain difficult to compare because existing models often differ in architecture, data, action space, and evaluation protocol. We present VLAFlow (Vision-Language-Action Flow), a unified flow-matching framework for controlled comparison of VLA training objectives. Using a heterogeneous robot corpus, OXEMix, containing approximately 5,000 hours of data from DROID, OpenX-Embodiment, OpenX-Augmented, and RoboCOIN, we evaluate four paradigms under the same pi0-style architecture, shared VLM backbone, action expert, and 14-dimensional action space: action-only modeling (MindPI), language-supervised co-training (MindLPI), future latent alignment (MindWPI), and their combination (MindLWPI). Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and SimplerEnv show that action-only pre-training is sensitive to heterogeneous data. In contrast, language supervision helps preserve vision-language generalization, while future latent alignment improves state-transition and action-outcome modeling. By combining both signals, MindLWPI achieves the most stable overall transfer performance across benchmarks. These results suggest a meta-action space view: language and future latent representations provide complementary intermediate constraints that make heterogeneous action supervision smoother and more transferable.

</details>


### [8] [Teaching Vision-Language-Action Models What to See and Where to Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01658)
*Yuguang Yang, Canyu Chen, Zhewen Tan, Yizhi Wang, Zichao Feng, Chunyang Liu, Kehua Sheng, Juan Zhang, Linlin Yang, Baochang Zhang, Yan Wang, Bo Zhang, Xianbin Cao*

TL;DR: DriveTeach-VLA 提出了一种面向自动驾驶的 VLA 训练框架，通过驾驶感知视觉蒸馏（DVD）注入驾驶先验知识，并利用 2D 轨迹引导提示（2D-TGP）提供空间条件，在 NAVSIM 和 nuScenes 上取得了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型过度依赖文本中心的 VQA 和 CoT 数据，强调语言推理而非动作导向的规划，导致学到的表征缺乏轨迹预测所需的空间依赖关系。

Method: 提出 DriveTeach-VLA 框架，包含三个组件：(1) Driving-aware Vision Distillation (DVD) 将驾驶感知先验注入视觉编码器；(2) 2D Trajectory-Guided Prompts (2D-TGP) 提供与可行驾驶轨迹对齐的空间条件；(3) 形成"看什么（DVD 预训练）→ 看哪里（TGP 监督微调）→ 如何行动（TGP 引导的 GRPO）"的视觉引导学习流水线。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 和 nuScenes 基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式教导 VLA 模型"看什么"和"看哪里"，可以有效提升自动驾驶中轨迹预测的可靠性，验证了动作导向视觉表征学习的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出DriveTeach-VLA框架，面向端到端自动驾驶中的轨迹预测，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Strong on world model and autonomous driving planning, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, autonomous driving, VLA training, vision distillation, trajectory guidance

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing VLAs' training relies heavily on text-centric visual question answering and chain-of-thought reasoning data, which emphasizes linguistic reasoning rather than action-grounded planning. As a result, the learned representations capture semantic knowledge but lack spatial dependencies crucial for reliable trajectory prediction. We propose DriveTeach-VLA, a framework that explicitly teaches VLAs what to see and where to look. Driving-aware Vision Distillation (DVD) injects driving-specific perceptual priors into the vision encoder, while 2D Trajectory-Guided Prompts (2D-TGP) provide spatial conditioning aligned with feasible driving trajectories. Together, they form a vision-guided learning pipeline: what to see (DVD pretraining) - where to look (TGP-guided SFT) - how to act (TGP-guided GRPO). DriveTeach-VLA achieves the state-of-the-art performance on NAVSIM and nuScenes. Our code is available at: .

</details>


### [9] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu, Zhaoqi Ma, Yicheng Chen, Wentao Zhang, Masaki Kitagawa, Zicen Xiong, Jinjie Li, Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (58.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoFL-S提出了一种低层级视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测机器人局部可见扇区上的语言条件流场（sector flow field）并滚动预测生成连续轨迹，在连续时间Habitat基准测试中优于动作token和动作chunk基线，并实现了零样本真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）研究多关注高层指令推理、记忆、全局地图构建和指令分解，而低层级动作表示（low-level action representation）相对未被充分探索。作者希望提出一种更好的低层级动作表示方法，以生成连续、平滑的轨迹。

Method: 提出CoFL-S框架：1) 将VLN-CE的整段指令-动作序列转换为帧级局部监督，包含对齐的子指令、匹配的动作、轨迹和稠密流场目标；2) 预测机器人局部可见扇区上的语言条件流场（sector flow field）；3) 通过滚动预测该流场生成连续轨迹。同时引入连续时间Habitat基准，使用共享速度指令控制器进行闭环评估。

Result: 在匹配编码器和训练设置下，CoFL-S在不同规划频率下均一致优于动作token和动作chunk基线；零样本真实世界闭环部署也展示了其相对于两个基线的优势。

Conclusion: CoFL-S通过语言条件流场表示低层级动作，在VLN任务中实现了更优的连续轨迹生成能力，验证了流场表示在具身导航中的有效性，并展示了从仿真到真实世界的良好迁移能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出CoFL-S，一个面向视觉-语言导航的低层级视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测语言条件流场生成连续轨迹，属于具身智能中机器人导航与动作规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on low-level navigation action representation, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: Vision-Language Navigation, continuous trajectory generation, sector flow field, zero-shot real-world deployment, low-level action representation

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [10] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo, Dongwon Kim, Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (86.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ACID提出了一种基于逆动力学的循环动作一致性（cycle action consistency）框架，用于改进世界模型驱动的决策时规划。通过将逆动力学模型从预测状态推断出的动作与原始条件动作进行一致性约束，并引入尺度不变自适应权重，ACID在6个任务和4种世界模型上一致提升了规划性能，同时大幅减少了规划计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于世界模型的决策时规划仅通过预测的终端状态与目标的接近程度来评估候选动作序列，忽略了中间状态转移的可实现性（realizability）。这导致预测轨迹看似合理，但实际环境 rollout 会偏离预测。作者希望引入一种机制来确保每个中间动作-状态转移的可实现性，从而提升规划质量。

Method: ACID框架在标准世界模型规划成本中引入循环动作一致性约束：给定一个预测的状态转移 (s_t, a_t, s_{t+1})，使用逆动力学模型从 (s_t, s_{t+1}) 反向推断动作 a'_t，要求 a'_t 与原始条件动作 a_t 一致。该一致性损失通过尺度不变的自适应权重（基于逆动力学预测误差的滑动统计）进行归一化，作为每步的残差项加入规划成本函数中。

Result: 在4种不同的动作条件世界模型（TD-MPC2、Dreamer、Iso-Dream、TAP）和6个任务（刚体/可变形物体操作、关节控制、视觉导航）上，ACID一致提升了规划性能，并在保持基线精度的同时显著减少了规划所需的计算量。

Conclusion: 循环动作一致性是一种简单而有效的正则化方法，能够提升世界模型规划中轨迹的可实现性，且具有模型无关性，可广泛适用于各类动作条件世界模型。该方法在提升性能的同时还能降低计算开销，具有实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ACID框架，通过逆动力学模型引入循环动作一致性约束来改进基于世界模型的决策时规划（decision-time planning），属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制。因此映射到[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）。

Importance reason: Strong world model planning improvement with cycle consistency, but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model planning, inverse dynamics consistency, planning compute reduction, embodied control, action-conditioned world models

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [11] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun, Jiajie Li, Yung-Hsu Yang, Chenyangguang Zhang, Tim Engelbracht, Sunghwan Hong, Cesar Cadena, Marc Pollefeys, Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (68.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LIME 提出语言条件相机运动生成任务，从第一人称视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督信号，结合自回归观测增益输出与连续流匹配位姿头，将被动人体视频转化为意图感知主动感知的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人常需移动相机才能执行任务（检查物体、揭示遮挡区域等），但语言条件相机运动作为一类动作尚未被充分研究。现有工作多关注导航指令翻译或操作动作映射，缺乏对语言驱动的相机运动生成建模。

Method: 从第一人称视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督，配对自然语言意图与相对 SE(3) 目标位姿。提出 LIME 模型，结合自回归观测增益输出（预测下一视图应揭示什么）与连续流匹配位姿头（表示多假设目标视图），实现语言条件相机运动生成。

Result: 实验表明 LIME 能从被动人体视频中学习主动选择相机位姿，在下游机器人任务中有效实现意图感知的主动感知。

Conclusion: LIME 验证了从被动第一人称视频中学习语言条件相机运动的可行性，为机器人主动感知提供了一种新的数据驱动范式。

Classification reason: 论文研究语言条件相机运动生成，从第一人称视频中学习意图感知的主动感知策略，属于机器人具身智能中的主动感知与相机规划，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and continuous multimodal generation priorities via flow-matching pose head and egocentric video supervision. Signals: world model, continuous flow-matching, egocentric video, language-conditioned camera motion, autoregressive observation-gain

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [12] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue, Le Xu, Zeyi Liu, Zhenyu Wu, Zhengyi Gu, Xinyang Song, Bofang Jia, Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (91.37) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WorldSample 是一个面向真实机器人强化学习的物理接地数据增强框架，通过后训练的世界模型生成高保真合成过渡数据，并引入策略节奏学习（PPL）来调节训练过程，在接触密集和精确操作任务上提升策略成功率28%并减少59%的训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人强化学习面临交互成本高的问题，每次物理 rollout 代价昂贵且仅反映一条动作-结果路径。现有方法依赖演示数据，但模仿学习受限于演示覆盖范围，而直接部署 RL 又因高交互成本而受限。

Method: WorldSample 构建了一个物理 rollout、世界模型生成与策略改进之间的真实-合成闭环。首先在真实 rollout 数据上后训练世界模型以生成高保真合成过渡数据；然后提出策略节奏学习（PPL），通过样本选择和调度来调节训练过程，平衡有用增强与价值过估计，并缓解幻觉引入的噪声。

Result: 在接触密集和精确操作任务上，WorldSample 相比基线将策略成功率提升28%，训练步数减少59%。同时，世界模型的视觉保真度在 PSNR 上提升19.4dB，SSIM 提升0.47，验证了真实-合成循环对策略和世界模型性能的有效性。

Conclusion: WorldSample 通过物理接地数据增强框架有效降低了真实机器人 RL 的交互成本，同时提升了策略性能和世界模型的视觉质量，为真实机器人强化学习提供了一种实用的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

Conclusion: CNeVA 成功实现了可控交通模拟智能体，在保持真实感的同时提供多维度的可解释控制。实验表明，引导指标必须与物理合理性护栏一起解读，以避免奖励破解混淆。该框架为自动驾驶测试中的可控模拟提供了有效工具。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable world models for simulation, a high-weight priority subtopic, with a novel latent-variable framework. Signals: world model, controllable generation, neural simulator, rectified flow, behavior latents

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present , a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

Result: ROF是预测闭环性能的最佳单一指标。CROF选出的世界模型训练的model-based A2C策略比model-free A2C基线高出约24.5分，且仅需约1/65的真实环境交互；该世界模型也驱动了强大的零样本CEM-MPC策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究潜空间世界模型（RSSM）的闭环性能预测与检查点选择，并应用于LunarLander环境中的MPC和基于模型的强化学习，属于具身智能中的规划控制范畴，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]。

Signals: World model checkpoint selection, LunarLander environment, RSSM model, Reward Observability Fraction, CROF metric

Abstract: We study how to predict the downstream closed-loop performance of a learned latent world model from validation-time diagnostics alone. Choosing the right checkpoint from a world-model training run is difficult: validation loss and multi-step prediction RMSE keep improving long after closed-loop performance has collapsed. We present a suite of structural validation-time diagnostics drawn from optimal-control theory and apply them to Gymnasium's LunarLander v3, which features shaped rewards. We train an RSSM [5, 4] world model on it and treat per checkpoint CEM-MPC return as the oracle for closed-loop quality. By evaluating 40 metrics against this oracle, we find that the strongest single predictor is the Reward Observability Fraction (ROF), which measures the reward predictor's dependence on the observable subspace. We combine ROF with three structural regularizers into a single-number offline checkpoint selection score, the Composite Reward Observability Fraction (CROF). The CROF-selected world model trains a model-based A2C policy that beats a fairly evaluated model-free A2C baseline by ~24.5 return points while using ~65x fewer real-environment interactions, and the same world model also drives a strong zero-shot CEM-MPC policy. TL;DR: Phi-Nav 提出一种统一在线策略框架，通过事后推理（hindsight reasoning）将智能体探索轨迹与语言指令对齐，利用三阶段双监督循环（oracle引导探索、事后指令生成、二次模仿学习）弥补在线策略中的语义监督鸿沟，在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 基准上以更少的专家演示取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略探索对训练鲁棒的视觉-语言导航智能体至关重要，但探索轨迹会偏离专家演示，导致视觉流与原始语言指令之间存在语义不匹配。现有方法缺乏有效手段将探索轨迹转化为有监督的训练信号。

Method: Phi-Nav 包含三阶段双监督循环：(1) oracle引导的在线策略探索，智能体采样轨迹并学习专家动作反馈；(2) 事后说话者（hindsight speaker）基于收集的视觉观测合成路径级事后指令；(3) 第二次模仿学习，将合成的轨迹-指令对作为额外专家演示进行训练。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 基准上，Phi-Nav 以更少的专家演示取得了与当前基线方法相竞争的性能，验证了语义探索在 VLN 中的必要性。

Conclusion: Phi-Nav 通过事后推理有效弥合了在线策略方法中的语义监督鸿沟，将语义未标记的探索运动转化为密集训练信号，为在有限数据下训练具身智能体提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉-语言导航（VLN）中的具身智能体探索与指令对齐问题，属于具身智能体在环境中进行动作规划和控制的核心范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied VLN with hindsight instruction alignment, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Vision-Language Navigation, on-policy exploration, hindsight reasoning, semantic supervision, embodied agent

Abstract: On-policy exploration is a crucial component for training robust Vision-Language Navigation agents, as it exposes the policy to a broader state distribution. However, such exploration inevitably leads to trajectories that deviate from expert demonstrations, resulting in a semantic mismatch between the executed visual stream and the original language instruction. In this work, we address this challenge by introducing Phi-Nav, a unified on-policy framework that leverages hindsight reasoning to align instructions with the agent's actual exploratory journey. Specifically, Phi-Nav operates through a three-stage dual-supervision cycle: 1) the agent performs oracle-guided on-policy exploration, sampling a trajectory while learning from expert action feedback, 2) a hindsight speaker synthesizes a path-level hindsight instruction grounded in the collected visual observations, and 3) the agent conducts a second imitation pass, treating the synthesized trajectory-instruction pair as an additional expert demonstration. Through this process, Phi-Nav bridges the critical semantic supervision gap inherent in on-policy methods, transforming semantically unlabeled movement into dense training signals. Evaluations on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks show that Phi-Nav yields competitive performance while requiring only a fraction of the expert demonstrations used by current baselines. These results underscore the necessity of semantic exploration in VLN, positioning Phi-Nav as an effective solution for training embodied agents with limited data.

</details>

Method: 提出PASE框架，包含三个核心组件：1) LLM作为Plan Synthesis Engine，从语义原语库生成结构化恢复计划；2) Neural-Symbolic World Model通过模拟验证计划可行性；3) Meta-Prompt Optimizer通过DRL学习生成最优提示以引导LLM规划过程。形成紧密的reason-plan-verify-adapt循环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上，PASE相比SOTA方法将平均系统恢复时间降低40%以上，并在未知故障场景中提高了故障检测准确率。

Conclusion: PASE通过统一LLM推理、模型辅助验证和元学习引导，实现了动态上下文感知的恢复策略生成，推进了自主系统管理的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个神经符号世界模型（Neural-Symbolic World Model）来验证LLM生成的云故障恢复计划，通过模拟验证计划可行性。这属于可交互与物理世界建模中的环境动力学建模与模拟器验证，与[interactive_physical_world]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Applies world model to cloud recovery, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model for verification, LLM planning for cloud, neural-symbolic approach, DRL for prompt optimization, practical engineering application

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>

Result: 在 PhysMani-Bench 基准以及真实机器人实验中，PhysMani 在动态物体操控任务上取得了显著优于现有强基线的成功率，验证了物理原则世界模型对动态操控的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个物理原则驱动的3D高斯世界模型用于动态物体操控，核心是物理一致的未来动力学预测与交互式操控，与 [CANONICAL] 可交互与物理世界建模 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and video generation priorities, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models. Signals: world model, 3D Gaussian, dynamic manipulation, physics-principled, robot manipulation

Abstract: Manipulating fast and dynamically moving targets in unstructured 3D environments remains challenging for embodied AI. Existing visual-language-action models and world models struggle with accurate 3D geometry and physically meaningful forecasting. We propose PhysMani, a framework that couples a physics-principled 3D Gaussian world model with a future-aware action policy model. The world model learns a divergence-free Gaussian velocity field via online optimization for fast and physically grounded future dynamics prediction. The policy model integrates the predicted 3D scene future dynamics through a learnable token based cross-attention module. We introduce PhysMani-Bench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark with 16 tasks, and demonstrate a superior success rate over strong baselines in both simulation and real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [20] [Certified World Models as Sensing Clocks: Drift-Aware Deadlines for Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01537)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (90.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出将认证世界模型（certified world model）作为"感知时钟"：利用世界模型预测的有效性期限来调度主动感知，决定智能体何时应停止"滑行"并重新感知。作者从经过审计的等变世界模型出发，推导出无感知间隔的截止期限，并证明可部署的截止期限必须考虑漂移（drift-aware）。在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型和合成基准上的实验验证了该方法在保持有效性的同时减少了事件尾部的违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动感知场景中，智能体需要决定何时重新感知环境以维持对世界状态的准确认知。现有方法通常依赖期望信念调度（expected-belief scheduling），但缺乏对世界模型预测有效性的正式保证。本文旨在将世界模型的认证（certification）转化为可操作的感知时钟，为主动感知提供有保障的调度规则。

Method: 从经过审计的等变世界模型出发，推导无感知间隔的截止期限。提出"漂移感知"（drift-aware）部署方法：仅靠流形上的Lyapunov率会高估滑行有效性，而校准的原生 rollout 漂移包络（calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes）能携带部署保证。在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型上验证，并在一个合成基准（cue-conditioned theorem-bed）中隔离调度规则进行对比。

Result: 在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型上，所提时钟在跨种子和数据分片的保留间隔上控制了证书违规。在合成基准中，该时钟在部署分布上保持有效，并在匹配感知预算下显著减少了事件尾部违规。在短视界冻结VN-JEPA场景中，经验共形视界与部署时钟在有效性和预算上匹配。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个认证感知时钟原语和漂移感知部署方法，为世界模型驱动的主动感知提供了形式化保证。作者明确指出，贡献在于方法本身而非声称谱时钟在所有非谱调度器上具有经验优势。

Classification reason: 论文提出了一种基于认证世界模型（certified world model）的主动感知调度方法，将世界模型的有效性预测转化为感知时钟，用于决定智能体何时需要重新感知。这属于世界模型中关于物理世界建模、环境动力学与交互式感知的范畴，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model certification for active perception, aligning with high-priority world_model and efficient inference directions. Signals: world model certification, drift-aware deadlines, active perception scheduling, VN-JEPA, Lyapunov rates

Abstract: Certified world models estimate how long their predictions remain valid. We turn this validity horizon into an operational sensing clock: a rule for when an agent should stop coasting and re-sense. Starting from an audited equivariant world model, we derive a deadline for no-sensing intervals and show that deployable deadlines in learned world models must be drift-aware: on-manifold Lyapunov rates alone overestimate coasting validity, while calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes carry the deployed guarantee. On a frozen 3D VN-JEPA model, the resulting clock controls held-out interval-simultaneous certificate violation across seeds and data shards. In a cue-conditioned theorem-bed (a synthetic bench where all schedulers share the exact model, isolating the scheduling rule), the clock remains valid on the deployment distribution and substantially reduces eventful-tail violations relative to exact-mixture expected-belief scheduling at matched sensing budget. We also report limits: in the short-horizon frozen VN-JEPA regime, empirical conformal horizons match the deployed clock on validity and budget, and a partial-reset exploration finds no clean budget-matched advantage for the spectral term. Thus the contribution is a certified sensing-clock primitive and drift-aware deployment method, not a claim that spectral clocks empirically dominate all non-spectral schedulers.

</details>

*David Courtis, Wenhao Li, Scott Sanner*

TL;DR: OPINE-World 提出一种 LLM 驱动的智能体，通过在线交互学习面向对象的程序化世界模型。它结合了环境交互智能体与代码合成智能体，利用反例引导归纳合成（CEGIS）和基于本体误差的贝叶斯探索策略，在 ARC-AGI-3 基准上以 78.4 的动作效率解决了 20/25 个游戏，无需逐游戏训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络世界模型数据需求大且泛化能力差，而程序合成世界模型虽数据高效但主要适用于结构化状态世界，难以扩展到像素渲染环境。本文旨在构建一个能从在线交互中灵活学习对象中心程序化世界模型的智能体。

Method: OPINE-World 包含两个协作智能体：一个在环境中执行动作并收集数据，另一个利用 LLM 合成世界模型代码，并通过回放验证和基于模型的规划进行迭代优化。采用贝叶斯本体误差度量（ontology error）引导探索，优先探索对象类型不确定性高的区域，结合 CEGIS 循环不断修正模型。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 基准上，OPINE-World 无需逐游戏训练即可解决 25 个游戏中的 20 个，动作效率得分达到 78.4（人类基线为 100），显著优于现有程序化世界模型方法。

Conclusion: OPINE-World 证明了将 LLM 驱动的程序合成与在线交互探索相结合，能够高效学习可迁移、可重用的对象中心世界模型，为在复杂像素环境中构建数据高效的世界模型提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出通过LLM智能体在线交互学习面向对象的程序化世界模型，属于可交互世界建模与物理环境动力学建模范畴，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and programmatic synthesis priorities, using LLM agents for interactive exploration and object-centric modeling. Signals: world model, programmatic world model, LLM agent, interactive exploration, ontology error

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>

### [22] [SUNTA: Hierarchical Video Prediction with Surprise-based Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02087)
*Tomoshi Iiyama, Masahiro Suzuki, Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 值得关注 (72.52) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SUNTA 提出了一种基于"惊讶信号"（预测误差）的分层状态空间模型用于长时序视频预测。通过解耦训练策略和内部不一致性作为顶层惊讶度量，该方法在 2D 和 3D 环境的视频预测任务中，能在 250 步内保持准确预测，而所有基线方法在前 10 步内就已退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层状态空间模型（HSSM）在长时序预测中依赖固定长度分块或相似性检测来划分时序边界，但这些方法往往与数据的固有时间结构不一致。作者认为分块应基于预测误差（惊讶信号），因为预测误差能更直接地指示何时需要更长程上下文。然而，将惊讶信号融入 HSSM 面临端到端训练中的层级崩溃和开环预测中缺乏惊讶信号等挑战。

Method: SUNTA（Surprise-based Nested Temporal Abstraction）采用解耦训练策略：先训练底层预测器，再基于底层预测误差训练顶层分块器，从而保留惊讶信号。在开环预测（想象 rollout）中，使用内部不一致性（internal inconsistency）作为顶层惊讶度量来确定分块边界。模型通过分层抽象实现长时序视频预测。

Result: 在 2D 和 3D 环境的视频预测任务中，SUNTA 显著优于基线方法。在 250 个时间步的长时序预测中，SUNTA 能保持准确预测，而所有基线方法在前 10 步内就已退化。消融实验验证了解耦训练和内部不一致性度量的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于惊讶信号的分层分块策略能有效提升长时序视频预测的性能。解耦训练和内部不一致性度量是解决惊讶信号在 HSSM 中应用的关键技术。该方法为长时序世界模型中的时序抽象提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于预测误差（surprise）的分层视频预测方法 SUNTA，用于长时序视频未来预测，属于视频世界模型中的未来预测与长时序视频建模方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "视频世界模型" (video_world_model)。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world model and video prediction priorities, with a novel surprise-based chunking method that significantly extends prediction horizon. Signals: world model, video prediction, hierarchical state-space model, surprise-based chunking, long-horizon prediction

Abstract: Hierarchical state-space models (HSSMs) offer a promising approach to long-horizon prediction by segmenting sequences into temporal chunks. However, their performance hinges on how chunk boundaries are determined. While prior HSSMs typically rely on fixed-length chunking or similarity-based boundary detection, these methods often misalign with the intrinsic temporal structure of the data. We argue that chunking should instead be driven by prediction errors, which more directly indicate when longer-range context becomes necessary. Nevertheless, integrating surprise-based chunking into HSSMs introduces critical challenges, including hierarchical collapse during end-to-end training and the absence of surprise signals during open-loop prediction. To address these issues, we propose Surprise-based Nested Temporal Abstraction (SUNTA), a method that employs a decoupled training strategy to preserve surprise signals and uses internal inconsistency as a top-down surprise metric to determine chunk boundaries within imagined rollouts. Experiments on video prediction tasks in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that SUNTA outperforms baselines, uniquely maintaining accurate predictions over 250 timesteps, whereas all baselines degrade within the first 10 timesteps.

</details>

*Hanlin Wang, Hao Ouyang, Qiuyu Wang, Wen Wang, Qingyan Bai, Ka Leong Cheng, Yue Yu, Yixuan Li, Yihao Meng, Zichen Liu, Yanhong Zeng, Yujun Shen, Qifeng Chen*

Conclusion: WorldDirector 通过解耦语义运动编排与视觉生成，成功实现了具有持久动态物体记忆和自由视角探索的高度可控视频世界模型，为构建更强大的世界模拟器提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 WorldDirector，一个可控视频世界模型框架，具备持久动态物体记忆和自由视角探索能力，属于视频生成式世界模型。同时其视频生成部分也匹配多模态生成方向。最佳匹配 [CANONICAL] subtopic: video_world_model（视频世界模型）。

Importance reason: Directly advances controllable video world models with persistent object memory, aligning with top-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model, video generation, controllable generation, persistent dynamic memory, LLM-coordinated 3D trajectories

Abstract: We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page:

### [24] [The Turning Point of 3D Plant Phenotyping: 3D Foundation Models Enable Minute-to-Second Cross-Crop Reconstruction and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01753)
*Hanyue Jia, Wei Zhou, Wenbo Zhou, Yanan Li, Hao Lu, Tingting Wu*

Main category: q-bio.QM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D基础模型（3DFM）的跨作物3D表型重建框架，将传统多视图重建流程从分钟级加速到秒级（6.52分钟→1.58秒），支持低质量手机视频/稀疏视图输入，并实现从几何重建到器官分割与表型测量的全流程自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D植物表型分析流程复杂、通量低，受限于多视图成像、脆弱的COLMAP重建管线以及从几何到表型提取的额外成本。低质量数据采集（如手机视频、稀疏视图）下这些问题更加严重。作者希望利用3D基础模型简化并加速这一流程。

Method: 用3DFM（3D Foundation Model）替代COLMAP风格的稀疏初始化进行前馈几何恢复，结合几何约束的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行稠密重建，通过迭代视图合成与细化实现少视图重建，并通过2D到3D语义迁移、度量尺度恢复和器官实例分割将重建几何转化为可测量的器官。同时构建了跨作物数据集（手机采集、多样形态、人工标注）。

Result: 在26个植物序列上，平均重建时间从6.52分钟降至1.58秒（加速约248倍），同时保持高重建质量和表型测量精度。

Conclusion: 3D基础模型为高通量3D植物表型分析提供了一条全新技术路线，从低成本图像采集到快速重建、感知、尺度恢复和表型测量均可实现。

Classification reason: 该论文利用3D基础模型（3DFM）和3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行植物表型的3D重建与器官分割，属于3D场景重建与生成范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D plant phenotyping, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords like "plant". Signals: 3D foundation models, cross-crop reconstruction, speed improvement, low-cost imaging, plant phenotyping

Abstract: 3D plant phenotyping is notoriously known to be procedure-complicated and of low throughput due to the extensive multi-view imaging, the fragile 3D reconstruction pipeline, and the additional cost from reconstructed geometry to phenotypic extraction. These limitations are further amplified in low-cost data acquisition, where smartphone videos or sparsely sampled multi-view images provide limited view overlap and self-occlusion. In this work, we show that the conventional 3D plant phenotyping pipeline could be streamlined and significantly accelerated with 3D Foundation Models (3DFMs), and particularly, present one of the first cross-crop 3D phenotyping frameworks powered by 3DFMs. The framework replaces COLMAP-style sparse initialization with 3DFM-based feed-forward geometric recovery, combines geometry-constrained 3D Gaussian Splatting for dense reconstruction, enables few-view reconstruction through iterative view synthesis and refinement, and converts reconstructed geometry into measurable organs through 2D-to-3D semantic transfer, metric scale recovery, and organ instance separation. We further construct a cross-crop dataset with smartphone-based image acquisition, diverse plant morphologies, and manual annotations for segmentation and phenotypic evaluation. Experiments across 26 plant sequences show that 3D Foundation Models reduce the average reconstruction time from 6.52 minutes to 1.58 seconds while maintaining high reconstruction quality and phenotyping accuracy. These results suggest a fresh technical route for high-throughput 3D plant phenotyping, from low-cost image acquisition to fast reconstruction, perception, scale recovery, and phenotypic measurement.

</details>


### [25] [PixGS: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Direct 3D Gaussian Splat Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01803)
*Duy Cao, Phong Nguyen-Ha*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: PixGS 是一种单阶段 3DGS 生成方法，利用像素空间扩散直接去噪 3D 高斯属性，避免了传统潜空间压缩带来的伪影和误差累积，在 1 秒内生成高质量 3D 资产，性能超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 生成方法通常依赖多级级联管道，将预训练的潜空间扩散模型适配到 3DGS 生成，但存在计算开销大、可扩展性差、潜空间压缩导致解码伪影和误差累积等问题。作者希望设计一种单阶段、高效且高质量的 3DGS 生成方法。

Method: PixGS 采用像素空间扩散模型，直接在像素空间中对 3D 高斯属性（位置、颜色、透明度、缩放、旋转等）进行去噪，避免潜空间压缩损失。同时引入综合监督策略，利用表面法线、深度和高频结构信息进行正则化，提升几何和外观质量。

Result: 实验表明 PixGS 在多个基准上优于当前最先进方法，推理速度仅需 1 秒（单张 A100 GPU），实现了高效且高质量的 3D 资产生成。

Conclusion: PixGS 证明了像素空间扩散在 3DGS 生成中的有效性，为 3D 内容生成提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案，避免了多级管道的复杂性和潜空间压缩的局限性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PixGS，直接从文本/图像生成 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS)，属于 3D 生成方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel single-stage pixel-space diffusion for 3DGS generation, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/video models. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splat generation, pixel-space diffusion, single-stage pipeline, fast inference (1s), bypasses latent compression artifacts

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D content generation from text or images have achieved impressive results, yet view inconsistency from 2D generators and the scarcity of high-quality 3D data remain significant bottlenecks. Existing solutions typically adapt large-scale pre-trained text-to-image latent diffusion models to generate 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS). However, these approaches often rely on training complex cascade pipelines that are computationally expensive and scalability-limited. Most critically, the quality of generated 3D assets is inherently constrained by each component capacity and compressed latent space, leading to decoding artifacts and accumulated errors. To address these limitations, we propose PixGS, a single-stage pipeline for direct high-quality 3DGS generation, which leverages recent advances in pixel-space diffusion to bypass lossy latent compression while still benefiting from the vast 2D generative priors. By directly denoising 3D Gaussian attributes at each timestep, our method enables precise, splat-level regularization of both appearance and geometry. Furthermore, we introduce a comprehensive supervision strategy that incorporates surface normals, depth, and high-frequency structural information, which is often overlooked in prior works. Experiments demonstrate that PixGS outperforms current state-of-the-art methods while maintaining a fast inference speed (1s on a single A100 GPU), offering a robust and efficient alternative to multi-stage generation pipelines.

</details>

*Clémentine Grethen, Florient Chouteau, Géraldine Morin, Simone Gasparini*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过知识蒸馏压缩 MASt3R 大模型（688M参数）用于月球立体三维重建，提出基于 SVD 的结构化初始化方法，在保持重建精度的同时将模型缩小7倍，并系统比较了不同编码器类型、解码器宽度/深度和训练策略对蒸馏效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型3D基础模型（如MASt3R）在立体重建上表现优异，但计算需求高，难以部署在硬件资源严格受限的场景（如行星探测的星载计算）。本文旨在探索这类几何基础模型能被压缩到何种程度。

Method: 以在月球图像上微调的 MASt3R（688M参数）为教师模型，蒸馏到轻量级学生模型。学生模型探索不同编码器类型（CNN vs ViT）、解码器宽度和深度。提出基于 SVD 的结构化初始化方法，将教师解码器权重投影到学生更小的潜空间，作为热启动。比较特征级蒸馏与输出级蒸馏的效果。

Result: 蒸馏后的学生模型在模型大小缩小7倍的情况下仍保留大部分重建精度，甚至优于直接用稀疏地面真值标注训练的基线。卷积编码器不如Transformer编码器；保留编码器容量比维持大解码器更关键；特征级蒸馏优于仅输出监督；SVD初始化改善优化稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏可以有效压缩几何基础模型用于资源受限环境下的3D重建，并总结出若干实用设计原则：优先保留编码器容量、使用特征级蒸馏、采用SVD初始化等。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于 MASt3R 大模型蒸馏的月球立体三维重建，属于 3D 场景重建方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on lunar 3D reconstruction via model distillation, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous language/multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: 3D reconstruction, model compression, knowledge distillation, lunar imagery, MASt3R

Abstract: Large 3D foundation models such as MASt3R achieve state-of-the-art stereo reconstruction but are computationally demanding for deployment under strict hardware constraints -- a critical limitation in domains such as planetary exploration, where onboard computing is severely restricted. We study how far such models can be compressed through knowledge distillation, using lunar stereo reconstruction as a challenging and practically relevant case study. Starting from a 688M-parameter MASt3R teacher fine-tuned on lunar imagery, we distill its dense geometric predictions into a family of lightweight students spanning different encoder types (CNN vs ViT), decoder widths and depths, and training strategies. To bridge the dimensional mismatch between teacher and student, we propose a structured SVD-based initialization that projects the teacher's decoder weights into the student's smaller latent space, yielding a warm start that significantly improves convergence and final performance. Based on our results on lunar data, we can obtain a distilled student that retains most of teacher's reconstruction accuracy while reducing the model size up to 7 times, and even outperforms a baseline trained directly with sparse ground-truth annotations. Beyond compression, our study highlights both principles and practical insights for distilling geometric foundation models: a convolutional encoder underperforms transformer-based alternatives (though pretraining availability remains a confounding factor), preserving encoder capacity is more critical than maintaining a large decoder, feature-level distillation consistently outperforms output-only supervision, and SVD-based initialization improves optimisation stability. These findings provide practical guidelines for deploying 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作感知方法在3D语义占用预测中面临通信开销与感知性能之间的权衡：压缩3D特征到2D会导致严重空间信息损失，而传输密集3D表示又阻碍实际部署。需要一种既能大幅降低通信带宽又能保留完整几何上下文的方法。

Method: 提出VQSOP框架，核心包括：(1) 稀疏感知向量量化（SAVQ）机制，利用3D场景的稀疏性仅对信息丰富区域进行紧凑编码，大幅降低通信开销；(2) 双分支自适应空间细化（ASR）模块，动态融合局部高频细节与全局上下文语义，增强结构一致性与特征连续性。

Result: 在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能，同时将通信量降低高达82倍。

Conclusion: VQSOP通过稀疏感知量化有效解决了协作3D语义占用预测中通信效率与感知精度的矛盾，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究3D语义占用预测（3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction），属于3D场景理解与重建范畴，与[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）方向高度相关。虽然论文侧重协作感知中的压缩传输，但其核心是3D空间结构的编码与重建，映射到该规范主题最为合适。

Importance reason: Focuses on collaborative 3D occupancy prediction with sparse quantization, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: 3D semantic occupancy, collaborative perception, vector quantization, bandwidth-efficient, sparse-aware

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends single-agent perception by enabling multiple vehicles to exchange complementary perceptual information. However, it introduces an inherent trade-off between perception gain and communication overhead, which is particularly severe for 3D semantic occupancy prediction that relies on fine-grained spatial structures. Existing methods typically compress 3D features into 2D, causing severe spatial information loss, or transmit dense 3D representations, hindering real-world deployment. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bandwidth-efficient collaborative Vector Quantization Semantic Occupancy Prediction (VQSOP) framework. VQSOP employs a Sparse-Aware Vector Quantization (SAVQ) mechanism that exploits 3D scene sparsity to compactly encode informative regions, drastically reducing communication overhead while preserving complete geometric context. Furthermore, to enhance structural consistency and feature continuity, we design a Dual-Branch Adaptive Spatial Refinement (ASR) module that dynamically fuses local high-frequency details with broad contextual semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing communication volume by up to 82x.

</details>


### [28] [PWM-ArtGen: Part World Model for Articulated Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02045)
*Wentao Zheng, Ancong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 值得关注 (75.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 PWM-ArtGen，一种基于部分世界模型（Part World Model）的统一框架，从单张图像生成铰接式3D物体。通过联合学习视觉动态与运动学参数的分布，并利用动作扩散与图像扩散的独立时间步耦合实现无标注数据的协同训练，在静止状态生成和零样本泛化上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从单张图像生成铰接式3D物体的方法存在两个主要问题：一是直接从静态图像推断运动学参数，缺乏动态部件级运动关系；二是先生成视觉动态再估计参数，存在两步累积误差。此外，标注数据规模有限，限制了泛化能力。因此需要一种能联合学习视觉动态与运动学参数、并能利用无标注数据的方法。

Method: 提出 PWM-ArtGen（Part World Model），将铰接物体建模为动态系统，统一学习视觉动态与运动学参数的联合分布。模型包含动作扩散分支和图像扩散分支，两者通过独立扩散时间步耦合，使得视觉分支可以在无运动学标注的数据上进行协同训练。同时构建了一个包含19.7k部件级图像对的无标注照片级真实数据集用于协同训练。

Result: 在静止状态生成任务上，PWM-ArtGen 显著优于现有基线方法，并在分布外物体上展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将铰接物体视为动态系统并联合建模视觉动态与运动学参数，结合无标注数据的协同训练，可以有效提升铰接式3D物体生成的准确性和泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从单张图像生成铰接式3D物体（articulated 3D object generation），属于3D生成范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel part world model for articulated 3D generation, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks boost keywords. Signals: 3D articulated object generation, part world model, joint visual dynamics and kinematics, unannotated data co-training, zero-shot generalization

Abstract: The key challenge in articulated 3D object generation from a single image is accurately predicting the underlying kinematic structure. Existing methods either infer kinematic parameters directly from a static image that lacks dynamic part-level kinematic relationships, or estimate parameters from visual dynamics generated from a single image, which is prone to accumulated errors of two steps. Moreover, the limited scale and diversity of existing annotated datasets further hinder generalization to complex, real-world objects. To overcome these limitations, we propose to learn the joint distribution of visual dynamics and kinematic parameters. Recognizing that articulated objects can be formulated as dynamic systems, we propose a unified Part World Model called PWM-ArtGen. To leverage unannotated data, this model couples action diffusion and image diffusion with independent diffusion timesteps, which enables visual branch co-training. We further curate a photorealistic dataset of 19.7k part-level image pairs without kinematic annotations, to support co-training. Experiments demonstrate that PWM-ArtGen substantially outperforms existing baselines in the resting state and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to out-of-distribution objects.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆渲染方法要么需要逐场景优化的高计算成本，要么基于图像空间的学习方法缺乏显式3D表示导致多视角不一致。本文旨在设计一种前馈式方法，在保持多视角一致性的同时高效恢复3D几何和物理材质属性。

Method: 将每个高斯原语参数化为均值、法线、不透明度、旋转、尺度、反照率、金属度和粗糙度，形成解耦的物理场景表示。模型集成材质估计网络的先验与多视角3D重建骨干网络，在单次前向传播中联合预测几何和反射率参数。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比2D基线具有更好的多视角一致性，准确的材质恢复，稳定的新视角渲染，并支持基于物理的重光照和视角依赖效应的忠实建模。

Conclusion: InvSplat 通过前馈式3D高斯预测实现了高效且多视角一致的逆渲染，为3D场景的材质恢复和重光照提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出前馈式多视角重建框架，直接预测带有材质属性的结构化3D高斯表示（3DGS），支持重光照和新视角合成，属于3D生成与重建方向，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel feed-forward inverse rendering with material attributes, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks autoregressive/diffusion generation focus. Signals: 3D Gaussian splatting, inverse rendering, feed-forward reconstruction, material estimation, novel view synthesis

Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to recover both 3D geometry and physically meaningful material properties from images, enabling applications such as relighting and novel view synthesis. Optimization-based methods achieve high fidelity but require costly per-scene fitting, while image-space learning-based approaches often suffer from multi-view inconsistencies and lack an explicit 3D representation for stable novel view rendering. We present a feed-forward multi-view reconstruction framework for inverse rendering that directly predicts a structured 3D Gaussian representation with intrinsic material attributes. Each Gaussian primitive is parameterized by mean, normal, opacity, rotation, scale, albedo, metallic, and roughness, enabling a disentangled and physically grounded scene representation. Our model integrates priors from a material estimation network with a multi-view 3D reconstruction backbone, allowing joint prediction of geometry and reflectance parameters in a single forward pass. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate improved multi-view consistency compared to 2D baselines, accurate material recovery, and stable novel view rendering. Our representation further supports physically-based relighting and more faithful modeling of view-dependent effects compared to existing RGB-based feed-forward reconstruction methods. Our project webpage is: $\href{ }{\text{ }}$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法（如 NeRF）在 RGB 新视图合成上表现出色，但扩展到多光谱、红外、偏振等非常规成像模态时，需要昂贵的专用传感器和标定设备来为每个新场景采集多模态数据。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈，使得仅从 RGB 图像即可合成多模态新视图。

Method: SPoILeR 采用两阶段训练策略：(1) 多模态预训练阶段，在包含 RGB 与多种非常规模态（红外、偏振、多光谱）的数据集上学习模态间的相互关联；(2) 微调阶段，仅使用目标场景的 RGB 图像作为监督，利用预训练学到的跨模态相关性预测非常规模态的多视角一致渲染结果。模型基于隐式神经表示架构。

Result: 实验表明，SPoILeR 能够在完全没有目标场景非常规模态传感器输入的情况下，准确渲染出红外、偏振和多光谱帧，在多个场景上取得了高质量的多模态新视图合成结果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过多模态预训练学习模态间相关性，可以在仅有 RGB 监督的条件下实现非常规模态的新视图合成，大幅降低了对专用传感器的依赖，为多模态神经渲染提供了更实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SPoILeR，一种基于神经隐式表示（NeRF-like）的多视角新视图合成方法，能够从 RGB 图像生成红外、偏振、多光谱等多模态渲染结果，属于 3D 场景重建与神经渲染范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题 "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel multimodal rendering approach but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Multimodal novel view synthesis, neural implicit representation, RGB-only supervision for non-RGB modalities, infrared/polarimetric/multispectral rendering, pre-training on multimodal correlations

Abstract: Neural rendering techniques allow for accurate reconstruction of the geometry and color appearance of 3D scenes. Some methods have extended their use to additional imaging modalities, such as multispectral, infrared, or polarimetric data. However, all of these approaches require expensive sensors and calibrated setups to capture new multimodal frames for each new scene. We propose Spectral and Polarimetric Implicit Learned Representation (SPoILeR), a novel method to obtain multi-view consistent renderings of unconventional modalities for scenes where either only RGB frames or very few of the additional modalities are available. Thanks to a multimodal pre-training phase, the model learns the mutual correlation between different modalities. This step allows predicting accurate renderings of unconventional modalities during a fine-tuning phase supervised only by RGB images. Experimental results show that the approach can accurately render infrared, polarimetric, and multispectral frames for scenes where no input sample captured by these types of sensors is provided.

</details>


### [31] [PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02515)
*Haofei Xu, Rundi Wu, Philipp Henzler, Nikolai Kalischek, Michael Oechsle, Fabian Manhardt, Marc Pollefeys, Andreas Geiger, Federico Tombari, Michael Niemeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointDiT 提出一种极简的像素空间扩散 Transformer，基于普通 ViT 架构，直接在原始 3D 点图 patches 上操作，以预训练 DINOv3 的图像 token 为条件，从零训练扩散骨干网络，无需点图 tokenizer。该方法在单目几何估计任务上超越了复杂的潜在扩散模型，且能生成更锐利的几何结构，对透明物体等高度模糊区域更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法通常依赖复杂的混合架构和损失函数，或将几何压缩到潜在空间以利用预训练的潜在扩散模型。作者认为这些架构开销和复杂的损失设计是不必要的，希望探索一种更简洁的像素空间扩散方法。

Method: 基于普通 ViT 构建像素空间 Diffusion Transformer（PointDiT），直接在原始 3D 点图 patches 上执行扩散过程，以预训练 DINOv3 提取的图像 token 作为条件。扩散骨干网络完全从零训练，无需点图 tokenizer 或预训练潜在扩散模型。

Conclusion: 单图像3D重建不需要复杂的混合架构和潜在空间压缩，简单的像素空间 Diffusion Transformer 即可取得更优结果，为单目几何估计提供了一种简洁而有效的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出单张图像深度/点图估计方法（单目几何估计），属于从单张图像重建3D几何信息，与3D生成与重建方向高度相关。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Strong pixel-space diffusion method for geometry, but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks key boost keywords. Signals: pixel-space diffusion, monocular geometry estimation, plain ViT architecture, sharper geometry, robust to transparent objects

Abstract: State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.

</details>

*Xianhui Meng, Zirui Song, Yuchen Zhang, Li Zhang, Yongxuan Lv, Xiuying Chen, Kun Wang, Yan Luo, Kai Chen, Hangjun Ye, Long Chen, Jun Liu, Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

TL;DR: SPG-Layout 是一个文本驱动的3D室内场景合成框架，专门针对非曼哈顿环境（非正交布局）设计。它利用物体分布统计先验指导训练，并采用分层布局策略（先放置大物体），在500个非曼哈顿环境基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的3D室内场景合成方法主要针对曼哈顿环境（正交布局），在非曼哈顿环境中难以建模非正交空间关系，导致几何违规和高物理保真度损失。本文旨在解决非曼哈顿环境下的物理合理室内场景生成问题。

Method: 提出SPG-Layout框架：(1) 利用物体分布的统计先验（Statistical Priors）指导训练过程，增强环境理解与保真度；(2) 采用分层布局策略（Hierarchical Layout），模仿人类设计流程，优先放置大物体，再放置小物体，从而最小化布局违规。同时构建了包含500个多样化非曼哈顿环境的基准数据集用于评估。

Result: 在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境上均进行了大量实验，SPG-Layout 在语义真实感和物理合理性方面一致且显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPG-Layout 通过统计先验和分层布局策略有效解决了非曼哈顿环境中的3D室内场景合成问题，为复杂非正交空间布局生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本驱动的3D室内场景合成，属于3D场景生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D scene synthesis, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks strong connection to user's key directions like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 3D indoor scene synthesis, non-Manhattan environments, hierarchical layout strategy, statistical priors, limited direct relevance to user's core interests

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>
