TL;DR: 本文提出了一个联合评测联邦学习（FL）和知识蒸馏（KD）在3D点云分类任务上的基准，涵盖13种FL算法×10种KD目标（130种组合），在ModelNet40和临床颅缝早闭数据集上进行了504次训练。主要发现：（1）极端非IID标签分布下FL性能严重下降；（2）KD可将教师模型压缩74.51%且速度翻倍，性能持平或超越教师；（3）FL+KD联合管线存在评测陷阱：使用硬标签交叉熵蒸馏时，即使教师完全崩溃（8.50%），学生仍可达92.94%，这反映的是代理标签而非教师质量。建议使用无标签蒸馏目标来真实反映教师质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感、资源受限的场景中部署3D点云分析面临两个障碍：数据无法集中，且模型需在有限边缘硬件上运行。现有工作缺乏对联邦学习与知识蒸馏在3D点云分类上联合效果的系统性评测。

Method: 构建了一个跨13种FL算法（FedAvg、FedProx、SCAFFOLD等）和10种KD目标（KL散度、MSE、Logit-MSE、关系KD等）的130种组合基准，在ModelNet40和临床颅缝早闭数据集上进行504次训练实验，系统分析非IID分布下的性能、模型压缩效果以及评测陷阱。

Result: （1）极端非IID下FL性能严重下降：ModelNet40上最佳方法76.32% vs 集中式92.26%；临床数据上最佳75.83% vs 100%。（2）KD成功将教师压缩74.51%，推理速度约翻倍，性能持平或超越教师。（3）发现评测陷阱：使用硬标签交叉熵时，崩溃教师（8.50%）+ Logit-MSE仍产生92.94%学生，84.4个百分点的差距反映的是代理标签而非教师质量。无标签蒸馏目标则与教师质量高度相关（r≈0.99）。

Conclusion: FL-KD联合管线存在严重的评测陷阱，使用含硬标签的蒸馏目标会掩盖教师模型的真实崩溃。建议在评测FL-KD管线时使用无标签蒸馏目标，使报告的学生准确率真实反映联邦教师模型的质量，而非代理标签。

Classification reason: 该论文对联邦学习与知识蒸馏在3D点云分类上的联合效果进行了系统性基准评测（504次训练、130种算法组合），属于生成模型/3D分析领域的评测基准工作，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on federated learning and knowledge distillation for point clouds, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Federated learning benchmark, Knowledge distillation, Point cloud classification, Non-IID label skew, Evaluation pitfall with hard labels

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud analysis in privacy-sensitive, resource-constrained settings faces two barriers: data cannot be centralized, and models must run on limited edge hardware. We present a multi-seed benchmark jointly evaluating federated learning (FL) and knowledge distillation (KD) for 3D point cloud classification. It spans 13 FL algorithms and 10 KD objectives (a 130-pair cross-product) across 504 training runs, evaluated on ModelNet40 and a clinical craniosynostosis dataset. We report three findings. First, under extreme non-IID label skew, standalone FL degrades sharply: on ModelNet40, the strongest method reaches 76.32% against a 92.26% centralized reference; on clinical data, the best reaches 75.83% against 100%. Second, distillation successfully compresses the teacher into a student 74.51% smaller and roughly twice as fast at inference, often matching or surpassing the teacher. Third, the combined pipeline exposes an evaluation pitfall: when distillation keeps a hard-label cross-entropy term on a labeled proxy split, a collapsed federated teacher (8.50%) paired with Logit-MSE still yields a 92.94% student. This 84.4-point gap reflects the proxy labels rather than the federated model, reusing the very labels whose privacy motivated federation. Objectives without hard labels instead track teacher quality ($r \approx 0.99$) and collapse when the teacher does. We therefore recommend evaluating FL-KD pipelines with label-free distillation so reported accuracy reflects the federated teacher, not the proxy.

</details>

### [66] [Overthink-Triggered Slowdown Attacks on LVLM-Based Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01518)
*Qiang Han, Jie Wu, Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

TL;DR: 本文系统性地识别并验证了针对LVLM机器人系统的"过度思考触发式慢速攻击"：攻击者通过在视觉场景中嵌入精心设计的场景文本，触发LVLM产生过长的推理链，从而显著拖慢机器人决策速度。在三个代表性LVLM上，单触发器最高可达6.96倍延迟放大，物理打印文本触发器仍可造成4.74倍延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在机器人系统中被广泛使用，但其"过度思考"行为（生成过长的推理链）会导致推理时间激增。攻击者可以利用这一行为，通过嵌入对抗性文本来故意触发过度思考，从而拖慢机器人决策，引发安全问题。目前缺乏对这种攻击的系统性研究。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(1) 构建推理密集型场景文本语料库，从短响应前缀中提取与过度思考相关的词汇特征；(2) 基于前缀代理分数进行高效黑盒搜索，对少量候选进行完整延迟测量确认；(3) 在未见图像和多个LVLM上评估黑盒迁移性，报告延迟放大比和攻击成功率。

Result: 在三个代表性LVLM上，所有触发器均产生大于1.0x的减速比，最强单触发器达到6.96x延迟放大。物理打印文本触发器仍可造成最高4.74x延迟放大。触发器在不同LVLM模型间具有迁移性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地研究了针对LVLM机器人系统的过度思考触发式慢速攻击，证明了通过嵌入对抗性场景文本可以有效触发LVLM过度推理，造成显著延迟。该攻击在黑盒设置下有效且具有跨模型迁移性，对LVLM在安全关键型机器人系统中的应用提出了重要警示。

Classification reason: 该论文研究针对LVLM机器人系统的"过度思考触发式慢速攻击"，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。论文通过嵌入对抗性场景文本来触发LVLM的过度推理行为，造成系统延迟，是一种针对多模态模型的新型对抗攻击。这与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on security attacks for LVLM robotics, which is outside your configured priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: LVLM security, overthinking attack, robotic system slowdown, black-box transfer, physical-world attack

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have been increasingly integrated into robotic systems. However, these models may exhibit overthinking behaviors, where they generate excessively long reasoning traces, incurring an excessive inference time. This overthinking behavior poses a serious risk to robotic systems, as the adversary can deliberately trigger overthinking to slow down the decision making of a victim robotic system, causing a variety of safety issues (i.e., an overthinking-induced slowdown attack). To initiate this attack, an adversary can embed carefully crafted, human-readable scene text into the visual scene observed by a victim robotic agent, causing significant inference delays even under a strict black-box setting. Therefore, the embedded scene text serves as a significant "trigger" for the attack. This work systematically identifies and validates transferable triggers of overthinking in robotic systems by introducing a three-stage framework. First, we construct a diverse corpus of reasoning-intensive scene text and extract overthinking-correlated lexical features from short response prefixes. Second, we perform an efficient black-box search guided by a prefix-based proxy score while selectively confirming a small set of top candidates with full latency measurements. Third, we evaluate black-box transfer using a fixed pool of triggers on unseen images and multiple LVLMs, reporting latency amplification and attack success rates under standard thresholds. Across three representative LVLMs, all triggers yield slowdown ratios greater than 1.0x, with the strongest single-trigger case reaching 6.96x. The physical printing of the text trigger still causes up to 4.74x latency amplification. These results demonstrate that our discovered triggers are transferred between multiple LVLM models and consistently cause significant slowdowns in robotic systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在工业原型和开源 VLM 上的实验表明，ISU-Test 相比随机场景生成基线，故障率提升高达10倍，故障覆盖率提升3.6倍，能有效发现 VLM 在车内场景理解中的缺陷。

Conclusion: 搜索式测试结合渲染场景生成是一种高效、可扩展的 VLM 测试方法，能够在早期设计阶段无需真实数据即可发现模型缺陷，为车载多模态系统的可靠性保障提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ISU-Test 方法，通过搜索式测试系统性地评估 VLM 在车内场景理解中的可靠性，属于多模态模型的安全性与鲁棒性测试范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM testing for in-car scenes, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: VLM testing, search-based testing, in-car scene understanding, rendering-based generation, software engineering

Abstract: In the automotive domain, in-car scene understanding (ISU) enables the detection of safety-critical events, such as driver distraction, and supports drivers or passengers by analyzing the in-car scene and adapting the environment (e.g., ambient lighting). The industry is increasingly exploring vision-language models (VLMs) to interpret camera-recorded in-car scenes and extract information for downstream reasoning tasks. However, VLMs may generate incomplete, erroneous, or misleading scene descriptions, highlighting the need for systematic testing. Collecting real in-vehicle data is costly, difficult to scale, and often infeasible, particularly in early design stages. In this paper, we present ISU-Test, an automated testing approach that combines rendering-based scene generation with search-based testing to evaluate ISU systems. By framing testing as an optimization problem and systematically modifying scene parameters, our method generates diverse in-car scenarios and explores a wide range of configurations. We evaluate ISU-Test on both an industrial prototype and open-source VLMs across two case studies: question answering and captioning, comparing against randomized scenario generation. Results show that ISU-Test significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving up to 10 times higher failure rates and up to 3.6 times higher failure coverage.

</details>

### [68] [ESC: Emotional Self-Correction for Reliable Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02089)
*Tien-Huy Nguyen, Minh-Nhat Nguyen, Nguyen Nhat Huy, Hung Viet Nguyen, Huy Nguyen Minh Nhat, Thanh-Huy Nguyen, Cuong Tuan Nguyen, Hoang M. Le, Dat Nguyen, Phat Kim Huynh, Min Xu, Ulas Bagci*

TL;DR: 本文提出ESC（情感自纠正），一种无需训练的VLM自纠正框架。通过外部验证器检测初始回答中的错误，并注入情感反馈（如鼓励谨慎思考）来触发模型自我反思和修正，在安全、幻觉、感知和多模态推理等多个基准上一致提升了可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在复杂多模态任务中仍存在不可靠推理的问题。已有的自纠正方法依赖后训练或精心设计的反馈，计算成本高。本文探索是否可以利用情感线索（emotional cues）激活VLM的潜在自纠正行为，无需额外训练即可提升推理可靠性。

Method: 提出ESC框架，包含一个外部验证器（verifier）用于检测初始回答中可能存在的错误，当检测到错误时，向VLM注入情感反馈（emotional feedback），鼓励模型进行更谨慎和反思性的推理，从而生成修正后的更优回答。整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在安全、幻觉、视觉感知和多模态推理等多个基准上，ESC一致提升了VLM的可靠性，同时保持了模型的整体效用。实验表明情感信号可以作为VLM中可扩展的自纠正控制信号。

Conclusion: 情感不仅可以作为VLM需要识别的能力，还可以作为实用的控制信号用于可扩展的自纠正。ESC为构建更可靠、类人且融合情感的多模态系统提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ESC（情感自纠正）框架，通过情感信号触发VLM的自我纠正行为，以提升多模态推理的可靠性和减少幻觉。这与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）高度相关，该子主题涵盖幻觉缓解、事实性、可靠性提升等方向。

Importance reason: Novel emotional self-correction approach but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: training-free self-correction, emotional feedback, VLM reliability, hallucination reduction, no direct link to continuous language or video generation

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to unreliable reasoning. Existing self-correction methods mitigate these issues but typically rely on post-training or carefully engineered feedback, incurring high computational cost. In this work, we revisit this challenge through the lens of emotional cues, asking whether they can activate latent self-correction behaviors in VLMs without additional training. \textbf{We find that emotional signals serve as an effective trigger for self-correction, encouraging more cautious and reflective reasoning}. Motivated by this finding, we propose \escabstract (\textbf{\underline{E}}motional \textbf{\underline{S}}elf-\textbf{\underline{C}}orrection), a training-free self-correction framework. ESC introduces an external verifier that detects potentially incorrect initial responses and injects emotional feedback to encourage model to reflect, and produce a better revised response without additional training. Extensive experiments across safety, hallucination, vision-centric perception, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that ESC consistently improves reliability while preserving overall model utility. These results suggest that emotion can function not only as an ability to be recognized, but also as a practical control signal for scalable self-correction in VLMs. \textbf{We therefore believe that ESC provides a strong foundation for a new reliable human-like, emotion-integrated research direction.} Our project is publicly available at \textcolor{red}{ }.

</details>


### [69] [LASER: A Corrective Lens for LVLMs via Visual Attention Preservation and Sink Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01707)
*Bowen Yuan, Zijian Wang, Yadan Luo, Shijie Wang, Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LASER提出一种后训练框架，通过视觉注意力保持和sink token抑制两个互补奖励，缓解LVLM在长序列解码中的视觉遗忘问题，在8个基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在长序列解码中会出现视觉遗忘，注意力逐渐偏离视觉证据。现有方法将其视为后期注意力衰减问题，但本文通过系统分析发现两个被忽视的因素：早期注意力衰减破坏了证据获取，以及注意力集中在少量与任务无关的视觉sink token上。

Method: 提出LASER后训练框架，包含两个互补奖励：Visual Grounding Reward（鼓励模型在整个解码过程中保持对语义显著视觉token的注意力）和Sink Suppression Reward（惩罚对视觉sink token的过度注意力集中），共同保持早期grounding并防止注意力坍缩到无信息区域。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上的实验表明，LASER一致性地优于强基线方法，验证了注意力感知训练作为视觉遗忘的有效缓解手段。

Conclusion: 视觉遗忘问题可以通过注意力感知的后训练框架有效缓解，早期注意力保持和sink token抑制是关键因素，为LVLM的可靠性提升提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LVLM在长序列解码中的视觉遗忘问题（注意力漂移、视觉sink token），提出LASER框架通过奖励机制保持视觉注意力并抑制注意力坍缩，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（视觉遗忘/幻觉缓解）范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses visual forgetting in LVLMs with a novel dual-reward post-training approach, but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: LVLM visual forgetting, attention preservation, sink token suppression, post-training framework, benchmark improvements

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) exhibit strong reasoning ability but suffer from visual forgetting during long-horizon decoding, where attention progressively drifts away from visual evidence. Existing methods largely treat this issue as a late-stage attention decay problem or attempt to mitigate it through heuristic reminders or post-hoc attention lifting. Through systematic empirical analysis, we find that performance degradation under visual forgetting is largely driven by two overlooked factors: early-stage attention decay disrupts evidence acquisition, and attention concentration on a subset of task-irrelevant visual sink tokens. Motivated by these insights, we propose LASER, a post-training framework that regulates both the visual attention trajectory and intra-visual token attention distribution during reasoning. Technically, LASER introduces two complementary rewards: a Visual Grounding Reward, which encourages the model to maintain attention on semantically salient visual tokens throughout decoding, and a Sink Suppression Reward, which penalizes excessive attention concentration on visual sink tokens. Together, these rewards preserve early-stage grounding while preventing attention collapse onto uninformative regions. Extensive experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that LASER consistently outperforms strong baselines, validating attention-aware training as an effective remedy for visual forgetting.

</details>

### [70] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang, Fangcong Yin, Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

TL;DR: 本文提出 VRRL 框架，通过强化学习训练视觉语言模型进行视觉接地自反思。核心创新包括随机掩码轨迹前缀和从经验回放缓冲区引入缓冲回滚，使模型学会从错误中间预测中恢复。在表格/图表视觉定位和空间导航任务上，该方法显著提升了分布外场景下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在思维链推理中具备自反思能力，但往往无法正确关注视觉输入，导致反思缺乏视觉接地，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。作者旨在通过强化学习训练框架，让模型学会基于视觉信息进行有效的自我纠正。

Method: 提出 VRRL 框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 随机掩码轨迹前缀——在训练时随机遮蔽部分推理轨迹前缀，迫使模型从错误的中间预测中恢复，而非仅关注早期错误；(2) 缓冲回滚——从经验回放缓冲区中引入多样化的失败状态，让模型学习如何纠正这些状态。整体采用强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在表格/图表视觉定位和空间导航基准上，VRRL 在分布外场景下显著优于标准 RL 和面向反思的微调基线，大幅提升了平均分布外准确率，而现成模型和传统微调模型在分布偏移下性能严重下降。

Conclusion: VRRL 框架通过强化学习有效增强了视觉语言模型在分布外场景下的视觉接地自反思能力，证明了通过精心设计的 RL 训练策略可以提升模型基于视觉信息进行自我纠正的能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于强化学习（RL）的训练框架 VRRL，用于增强视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉接地自反思能力，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Focuses on visual grounding self-reflection via RL, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models or video/image generation. Signals: multimodal_understanding_generation, reinforcement learning, visual grounding, self-reflection, distribution shift

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [71] [SkillCoach: Self-Evolving Rubrics for Evaluating and Enhancing Agentic Skill-Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01874)
*Jiayin Zhu, Kelong Mao, Yudong Guo, Dengbo He, Sulong Xu, Simiu Gu, Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SkillCoach 提出一种自演化评分标准框架，用于评估和增强 LLM agent 的技能使用能力。它从真实 rollout 中推导出基于技能的过程评分标准，从技能选择、遵循、组合和反思四个维度评估轨迹，并将过程质量与最终任务成功解耦。演化后的评分标准可作为过程监督信号，用于筛选高质量训练轨迹，显著提升评估质量和训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实的技能库中，技能之间存在重叠，使得 agent 可靠地使用技能变得困难。仅依赖最终验证器的成功/失败信号过于粗糙，无法区分 agent 是通过试错偶然成功还是真正掌握了技能使用，也无法暴露技能选择错误、步骤遗漏、组合错误等问题。

Method: SkillCoach 框架包含四个维度（技能选择、技能遵循、技能组合、技能反思）的过程评分标准，从真实 rollout 轨迹中自动演化生成。它将外部验证器作为独立的结果信号保留，从而区分过程质量与偶然任务成功。演化后的评分标准进一步作为过程监督信号，用于从训练数据中筛选高质量轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，演化后的评分标准显著提升了评估质量，能够暴露被最终准确率掩盖的失败模式，并且相比仅基于结果过滤的方法，提供了更强的监督信号来增强 agent 的技能使用能力。

Conclusion: SkillCoach 通过自演化过程评分标准，有效解耦了过程质量与结果成功，为 LLM agent 的技能使用评估和训练提供了更细粒度的监督信号，是一种有前景的 agent 能力提升方法。

Classification reason: 该论文关注 LLM agent 的技能使用评估与训练，通过自演化评分标准（rubrics）提供过程监督信号以筛选高质量训练轨迹，属于多模态/LLM 强化学习与对齐范畴（偏好学习、过程监督、训练数据筛选），因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM agent skill evaluation, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Agent skill-use evaluation, Self-evolving rubrics, Process supervision, LLM agents, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Skills are becoming a reusable operational layer for LLM agents, encoding SOPs, domain rules, tool workflows, scripts, and validation routines. In realistic skill repositories, overlapping skills make reliable skill-use difficult. Final verifier success is too coarse for both evaluation and training, since an agent may pass through trial and error while selecting distractor skills, skipping required steps, composing workflows incorrectly or omitting final checks. We introduce SkillCoach, a self-evolving rubric framework for evaluating and enhancing agentic skill-use. SkillCoach derives skill-grounded process rubrics from real rollouts and evaluates trajectories along four dimensions: skill selection, skill following, skill composition, and skill-grounded reflection. It keeps the external verifier as a separate outcome signal, allowing process quality to be distinguished from accidental task success. The evolved rubrics further serve as process supervision for selecting high-quality training trajectories. Experiments show that evolved rubrics substantially improve evaluation quality, expose failures hidden by final accuracy, and provide stronger supervision signals than outcome-only filtering for enhancing agentic skill-use.

</details>


### [72] [Multimodal Knowledge Edit-Scoped Generalization for Online Recursive MLLM Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01978)
*Siyuan Li, Youyuan Zhang, Ruitong Liu, Junxi Wang, Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (70.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScopeEdit，一种面向在线递归多模态大模型编辑的范围感知方法。通过将每次编辑分解为模态局部吸收分支和证据门控共享泛化分支，在正交低秩空间中实现范围分离的写入几何，有效控制了编辑的跨模态传播边界，在保持编辑可靠性的同时提升了范围内跨模态迁移与范围外局部性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线多模态知识编辑方法主要关注编辑可靠性和长期稳定性，但忽略了每次编辑的语义边界控制。实验发现，实例级别的编辑成功并不能保证有效迁移到有效的跨模态变体，也无法防止泄漏到无关输入。因此需要一种能够控制编辑传播边界的方法。

Method: ScopeEdit 将每次更新分解为两个分支：(1) 模态局部吸收分支，负责稳定吸收编辑；(2) 证据门控共享泛化分支，仅在视觉和文本证据充分对齐时才允许跨模态传播。两个分支在正交低秩空间中执行范围分离的写入几何，并通过 Sherman-Morrison 递归维护分支级预条件子，保持每次编辑的恒定开销。

Result: 在多种基准、长序列编辑流、不同 MLLM 骨干网络、真实 VLKEB 场景以及复杂视觉语言架构上的实验表明，ScopeEdit 一致地改善了范围内跨模态迁移与范围外局部性之间的权衡，同时保持了编辑可靠性、稳定性和在线效率。

Conclusion: ScopeEdit 通过显式建模编辑的语义传播边界，有效解决了在线多模态知识编辑中的"范围差距"问题，为多模态大模型的可控知识更新提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型的知识编辑（knowledge editing），核心是控制编辑的传播边界以实现跨模态泛化与无关行为保持之间的平衡。这属于多模态模型的对齐与可控性优化范畴，与现有 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）中关于多模态对齐、偏好学习、可控性的主题高度相关。虽然该论文不直接使用 RLHF/DPO，但其"scope-aware editing"本质上是对多模态模型行为进行精细控制与对齐，因此映射到此 subtopic 最为合适。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM editing, which is not a priority subtopic, and uses negative keywords like "multimodal" without boosting core interests. Signals: MLLM editing, scope-aware generalization, online recursive editing, cross-modal propagation, orthogonal low-rank spaces

Abstract: Online multimodal knowledge editing requires injecting a continual stream of visual-textual corrections into multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with bounded overhead and minimal disruption to unrelated behaviors. Existing editors mainly emphasize edit reliability and long-horizon stability, but rarely control the semantic boundary of each edit. Our pilot analyses of post-edit behaviors and internal neuronal activities reveal a scope gap behind reliable edits: instance-level success neither guarantees transfer to valid cross-modal variants nor prevents leakage to unrelated inputs, while edit-related cross-modal responses concentrate in deeper semantic layers. Therefore, we formulate Edit-Scoped Generalization, reframing online MLLM editing from merely correcting an instance to controlling the propagation boundary of each edit. To this end, we propose ScopeEdit, a scope-aware online editor that decomposes each update into a modality-local absorption branch and an evidence-gated shared generalization branch. The local branch supports stable edit absorption, whereas the shared branch enables cross-modal propagation only when visual and textual evidence are sufficiently aligned. Both branches perform scope-separated write geometries in orthogonal low-rank spaces and maintain branch-wise preconditioners via Sherman--Morrison recursions, yielding constant per-edit overhead. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, long-horizon edit streams, MLLM backbones, real-world VLKEB scenarios, and complex vision-language architectures show that ScopeEdit consistently improves the trade-off between in-scope cross-modal transfer and out-of-scope locality, while preserving edit reliability, stability and online efficiency. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [73] [Rank-Then-Act: Reward-Free Control from Frame-Order Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01897)
*Yuriy Maksyuta, George Bredis, Ruslan Rakhimov, Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Rank-Then-Act (RTA) 框架，利用 VLM 从专家视频演示中学习基于进度的排序评分器，并通过 Spearman 秩相关构造奖励信号，无需环境奖励即可进行策略学习，在离散和连续控制任务上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习需要精心设计的奖励函数，这在复杂任务中难以获取。从视频演示中学习奖励是一种有前景的替代方案，但现有方法通常需要绝对校准的奖励模型，泛化能力有限。本文旨在设计一种无需环境奖励、可跨任务迁移的奖励学习框架。

Method: RTA 框架包含两个阶段：(1) 离线训练阶段：使用 GRPO 目标在打乱的帧序列上训练 VLM 作为基于进度的排序评分器，迫使模型从视觉语义中恢复时序顺序；(2) 在线 RL 阶段：在每个交互窗口，计算预测进度排名与真实时间索引之间的 Spearman 秩相关系数作为奖励信号，该信号有界且尺度不变，无需绝对校准。在离散控制（PyBoy 游戏）和连续控制（PointMaze、MetaWorld）任务上评估。

Result: RTA 在 Catrap、Kirby、PointMaze、MetaWorld 等基准上一致匹配或超越先前的基于视频的奖励学习方法（如 VIP、R3M、LIV）和基于排名的基线方法，并展示了单个预训练进度评分器在跨任务复用时的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于视频时序序号的秩相关结构化监督信号足以用于策略学习，为无需显式奖励设计的可扩展强化学习提供了一种有效替代方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 VLM 结合 GRPO（一种强化学习目标）进行奖励学习，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，与 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Uses VLM for reward-free control, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM-based progress scorer, Spearman rank correlation reward, offline GRPO training, cross-task transfer, discrete and continuous control

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Then-Act (RTA), a framework for learning control policies from expert video demonstrations without environment rewards. RTA trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) offline as a progress-based ordinal scorer, using a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective over shuffled frame sequences, which forces the model to recover temporal ordering from visual semantics rather than trivial time cues. Importantly, instead of using the scorer directly as a scalar reward model, we propose a correlation-based reward function for reinforcement learning: at each interaction window, we compute the Spearman rank correlation between predicted progress rankings and true temporal indices, yielding a bounded, scale-invariant learning signal. This design decouples reward learning from absolute calibration and enables stable transfer across tasks and environments. We evaluate RTA on discrete control benchmarks (PyBoy: Catrap, Kirby) and continuous control tasks (PointMaze, MetaWorld). RTA consistently matches or outperforms prior video-based reward learning methods and rank-based baselines, while demonstrating strong cross-task reuse of a single pretrained progress scorer. Our results suggest that correlation-structured supervision over video-derived ordinal signals is sufficient for policy learning, offering a scalable alternative to explicit reward design.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth, Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski, Sam Adam-Day, Adam Gleave, Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文扩展了SOLiD（可扩展监督谎言检测器）方法到更大规模模型（最高405B参数），发现其具有有利的扩展趋势——未检测到的欺骗率从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%。同时发现可以完全移除昂贵的人工标注者而不显著增加欺骗率，但SOLiD对训练数据分布偏移敏感，可能导致误报率过高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的欺骗行为监控成本高昂，现有方法难以在更大模型上有效扩展。需要一种可扩展的监督方法来检测和减少偏好学习中的欺骗行为。

Method: 将SOLiD方法扩展到更大规模（1B到405B参数）的模型上，在更多样化和真实的偏好学习场景中进行评估。SOLiD使用谎言检测器识别需要由高成本标注者审查的响应，从而在有限人工监督下实现可扩展的欺骗监控。

Result: 未检测到的欺骗率从1B模型的34%下降到405B模型的14%（检测器真阳性率99%）；可以完全移除微调阶段的人工标注者而不显著增加欺骗率；但SOLiD对训练数据分布偏移敏感，可能导致检测器假阳性率过高到不实用的水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD在更大模型上展现出有利的扩展趋势，但分布偏移问题需要进一步解决才能在实际中可靠部署。

Classification reason: 该论文研究偏好学习（preference learning）中的可扩展监督方法（SOLiD），使用谎言检测器来监控LLM的欺骗行为。这属于多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）范畴下的偏好学习与对齐方向，尽管论文聚焦于文本LLM而非多模态，但其方法论（偏好学习、RLHF风格的对齐）与该子主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM deception detection scaling, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Scaling trends for lie detector oversight, deception rate drops from 34% to 14%, human labelers can be removed, sensitive to distribution shift, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [75] [Hidden Forgetting in Continual Multimodal Learning: When Accuracy Survives but Grounding Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02020)
*Qianyu Chen, Canran Xiao, Runxuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (78.48) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现持续多模态学习中存在"隐藏遗忘"现象：模型虽能保持答案准确率，但悄然改变了所依赖的证据通道（如从视觉转向文本）。作者提出无回放的依赖约束持续学习框架 RCL，通过冻结旧模型作为行为参考，利用反事实通道干预估计师生证据依赖分布，联合优化任务学习、预测保持和依赖保持，有效降低了证据依赖漂移和隐藏遗忘率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习指标仅关注答案正确性是否保持，忽略了多模态模型在持续适应过程中对视觉、文本、OCR、图表等证据通道的使用稳定性。这种"隐藏遗忘"——答案正确但证据依赖悄然改变——可能导致模型在分布外场景下不可靠，亟需被检测和缓解。

Method: 提出 RCL（Reliance-Constrained Continual Learning）框架：1）冻结旧 checkpoint 作为教师行为参考；2）通过反事实通道干预（遮挡各模态通道）估计教师和学生的证据依赖分布；3）联合优化三个目标——任务学习损失、预测保持损失（KL 散度）和依赖保持损失（证据分布 KL 散度）。无需回放数据，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在 CoIN、COAST、MCITlib 和自建证据敏感多模态流上，RCL 在最终性能和遗忘率上一致优于无回放、PEFT、路由和记忆辅助基线。显著降低了模态依赖漂移、主导证据翻转率和隐藏遗忘率，同时保持了答案准确率。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的持续多模态学习不仅需要保持答案正确性，更需要保持答案背后的证据路径。RCL 通过约束证据依赖分布有效缓解了隐藏遗忘，为多模态模型的持续适应提供了更可靠的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究持续多模态学习中模型对视觉、OCR、图表等证据通道的依赖稳定性，提出约束证据依赖的持续学习框架，属于多模态对齐与偏好学习范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）涵盖多模态对齐与偏好优化，与此论文的"证据依赖对齐"目标高度吻合。

Importance reason: Introduces hidden forgetting in continual multimodal learning, relevant to multimodal understanding but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: continual multimodal learning, hidden evidence-use forgetting, replay-free framework, multimodal grounding stability, counterfactual channel interventions

Abstract: Multimodal large language models must continually adapt to evolving tasks and domains, yet standard continual learning metrics mainly measure whether old answers remain correct, leaving the stability of multimodal grounding largely unexamined. We study this overlooked failure mode and ask whether a continually adapted MLLM can preserve not only what it answers, but also how it uses visual, textual, OCR, chart, and document evidence. We identify \emph{hidden evidence-use forgetting}, where answer accuracy is retained while the model silently shifts toward different or less grounded evidence channels, and propose \textsc{RCL}, a replay-free reliance-constrained continual learning framework. \textsc{RCL} freezes the previous checkpoint as a behavioral reference, estimates teacher and student evidence-reliance profiles through counterfactual channel interventions, and jointly optimizes task learning, prediction preservation, and reliance preservation without adding inference-time cost. Across CoIN, COAST, MCITlib, and an evidence-sensitive multimodal stream, \textsc{RCL} consistently improves final performance and reduces forgetting over replay-free, PEFT, routing, and memory-assisted baselines, while substantially lowering modality reliance drift, dominant evidence flips, and hidden forgetting rates. These results suggest that robust continual multimodal learning requires preserving the evidence path behind correct answers, not merely the answers themselves.

</details>

### [76] [EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02479)
*Jingtao Xu, Zizhuo Lin, Jianwen Sun, Yi Yang, Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EAGLE-360提出了一种面向360°全景环境的主动全局到局部探索框架，通过RoPE Rolling位置编码建模全景连续拓扑，结合SFT与GRPO训练，在360°视觉搜索任务上实现约8倍准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLLM在360°全景环境中难以有效建模极地畸变和连续圆柱拓扑等全景特性，导致目标检测精度下降。现有全景搜索方法依赖碎片化局部视角，缺乏全局先验，存在短视、低效探索和错误恢复困难等问题。

Method: 提出EAGLE-360框架，采用全局到局部（Global-to-Local）的迭代推理策略：先利用全局先验建立初始全景视角，再逐步缩小搜索空间。架构上引入RoPE Rolling坐标偏移位置编码来建模全景连续拓扑。构建了包含14000+张4K全景图和70000+轮高质量VQA对话的大规模数据集，采用SFT+GRPO训练流程激发复杂空间推理和工具调用能力。

Result: 在360°视觉搜索任务上达到新SOTA，相比基模型准确率提升近8倍，同时显著提高了探索效率。

Conclusion: EAGLE-360通过全局到局部探索范式、RoPE Rolling位置编码和SFT+GRPO训练，有效解决了MLLM在360°全景环境中的视觉搜索难题，验证了结合空间推理与强化学习优化策略的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究360°全景环境中的主动视觉搜索，核心是MLLM的空间推理与工具调用能力，属于多模态推理范畴。论文使用SFT+GRPO训练提升复杂空间推理能力，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel 360° exploration framework but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: 360° visual search, RoPE Rolling, GRPO training, panoramic exploration, MLLM spatial reasoning

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.

</details>

### [77] [FlowCIR: Semantic Transport via Flow Matching for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02284)
*Zhenqi He, Ziqi Jiang, Yuanpei Liu, Yanghao Wang, Teng Wang, Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: FlowCIR 提出将零样本组合图像检索建模为条件语义传输问题，利用条件流匹配学习一个轻量级传输场，将指令表示映射到目标对齐的查询嵌入，相比基于文本反转的方法训练效率提升约10倍，并提出了多负向引导策略处理否定语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本组合图像检索方法依赖文本反转将参考图像转为伪文本token，再与指令简单拼接，这种方式在细粒度语义上存在信息损失且脆弱。作者希望提出一种新范式，直接在嵌入空间进行语义传输，避免离散文本空间的语义损失。

Method: FlowCIR 将 ZS-CIR 建模为条件语义传输：利用条件流匹配学习一个轻量级传输场，以参考图像嵌入为条件，将指令表示逐步映射到目标对齐的查询嵌入。模型仅训练一个小型传输模块，不更新图像或文本编码器。此外提出推理阶段的多负向引导策略，通过将包含否定的相对指令从其否定语义方向推开，增强对否定查询的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个标准 CIR 基准上，FlowCIR 取得了与近期 ZS-CIR 方法相比有竞争力的性能，训练资源需求约为先前方法的1/10。

Conclusion: FlowCIR 验证了条件流匹配在组合图像检索中的有效性，提供了一种训练高效、语义保真的新范式，并揭示了 VLM 在否定语义处理上的局限性，提出了有效的推理时缓解策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR），属于跨模态/组合检索任务，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on composed image retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic, and lacks strong alignment with user's key directions like generation or world models. Signals: zero-shot composed image retrieval, conditional flow matching, training efficiency, negation handling, not a priority subtopic

Abstract: Zero-shot composed image retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image by editing a reference image with a natural-language instruction, without relying on domain-specific annotated triplets. Most existing ZS-CIR methods rely on textual inversion to translate the reference image into pseudo-text tokens and then compose them with the instruction via simple concatenation in the text space, which can be lossy and brittle for fine-grained semantics. In this work, we propose a new paradigm, namely FlowCIR, that casts ZS-CIR as conditional semantic transport between reference and target embeddings. Leveraging \emph{conditional flow matching}, our model learns a lightweight transport field that maps the instruction representation toward a target-aligned query embedding conditioned on the reference image. Since FlowCIR operates on pre-extracted VLM embeddings and trains only a small transport module without updating the image or text encoder, it offers a computationally efficient training protocol compared with prior textual-inversion-based approaches. The resulting framework is training-efficient, requiring roughly $10\times$ fewer training resources than prior textual-inversion-based approaches. We further identify negation and removal as a major failure mode of VLM-based composition. To address this, we propose an inference-only Multi-Negative Steering strategy that steers a negation-containing relative instruction away from its negated semantics, mitigating the limited negation handling of VLMs and improving robustness on negation-heavy queries. Extensive experiments on standard CIR benchmarks demonstrate that FlowCIR achieves strong and competitive performance compared with recent ZS-CIR methods.

</details>

### [78] [Office Comprehension Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01245)
*Firoz Shaik, Mateus Picanço Lima Gomes, Tanvir Aumi, Jingci Wang, Milos Milunovic, Filip Basara, Ivana Jovanovic, Vishwas Suryanarayanan, Neha Nandan Kenkare, Weiyao Xie, Zhipeng Han, Zheng Zhang, Waleed Shahid, Jay Rathi, Russell Scherer, Thong Q. Nguyen, Michael Bentley, Tamara Stankovic, Rasika Chakravarthy, Vishal Chowdhary*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CY, cs.AI, cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Office Comprehension Bench (OCB)，首个公开的评估LLM对Word、Excel、PowerPoint原生文件格式理解能力的基准。包含文件保真度问答和领域问答两个赛道，将参考答案分解为原子化可二值评分的声明，使用LLM评委集成进行评分。最强模型在默认推理模式下仅达59.3%，增加推理深度无明显提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测主要关注纯文本或图像理解，缺乏对办公文档（Word/Excel/PPT）原生格式的结构化与视觉理解能力的系统评估。办公文档包含表格、图表、公式、页眉、演讲者备注等复杂元素，对模型的结构感知和视觉感知能力提出了独特挑战。

Method: 构建OCB基准，包含两个赛道：(1) 文件保真度问答——测试对表格、图表、嵌入图像、公式、页眉、演讲者备注、命名区域等办公文档元素的感知能力；(2) 领域问答——覆盖12个专业领域的真实行业文档，需要多步分析和跨文档综合推理。参考答案被分解为原子化的可二值评分声明，使用LLM评委集成对每个声明独立评分。

Result: 最强前沿系统在默认推理模式下仅达到约59.3%的领域问答准确率；增加同一产品层级内的推理深度对性能提升有限，升级到更高产品层级带来小幅提升。

Conclusion: 办公文档理解对当前最强LLM仍是一个重大挑战，现有模型在该任务上表现有限，且简单增加推理深度或升级模型层级并不能带来显著改善，表明需要更根本的方法创新。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了Office Comprehension Bench (OCB)，一个评估LLM对Word/Excel/PPT原生文件格式理解能力的新基准。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因为OCB评测了模型对包含表格、图表、图像、公式等视觉元素的办公文档的理解能力，是一个新的多模态评测基准。

Importance reason: This benchmark is valuable for office document understanding but is not aligned with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: new benchmark, office document comprehension, LLM evaluation, low relevance to user priorities, no generative or continuous modeling focus

Abstract: We introduce Office Comprehension Bench (OCB), the first public benchmark to jointly evaluate LLM systems on Word, Excel, and PowerPoint comprehension over native file formats (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx) and their variants. OCB consists of two tracks. File Fidelity Q&A tests structural and visual perception of office artifacts - tables, charts, embedded images, formulas, and app-specific elements such as headers, speaker notes, and named ranges. Domain Q&A tests expert-level reasoning grounded in real-world industry documents across 12 professional domains, with queries requiring multi-step analysis and synthesis across documents. Each reference answer is decomposed into atomic, binary-gradable claims, and an ensemble of LLM judges scores responses against each claim independently. Even the strongest frontier system in its default reasoning mode reaches only about 59.3% on Domain Q&A; increasing thinking depth within a tier does not move performance materially, while moving to a higher product tier yields modest gains. We release the dataset, evaluation tooling, judge prompt, and a public leaderboard.

</details>


### [79] [RusFinChain: A Russian Benchmark for Verifiable Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Finance with Fuzzy-Aligned Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01388)
*M. K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RusFinChain是首个俄语金融领域可验证思维链推理基准，包含5280个参数化示例和自动验证的推理链，并引入模糊数值对齐和软注意力对齐两种增强指标。在8个开源LLM上的评估显示模型在步骤对齐上Hard F1约0.65，但最终答案正确率仅约29%，揭示了显著的推理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融推理基准（如FINCHAIN仅限英语，FINESSE-Bench的俄语部分缺乏步骤级监督）无法满足俄语金融场景下可验证思维链推理的评估需求，因此需要构建一个俄语、可验证、抗污染的金融推理基准。

Method: 从可执行Python模板生成5280个参数化示例，覆盖17个领域172个主题，每个示例包含标准答案推理链和中间数值。提出Fuzzy Numeric Alignment和Soft-Attention Alignment两种增强指标，用于更细粒度地评估推理步骤对齐质量。在8个开源LLM上采样8100个响应进行评测。

Result: 模型在步骤对齐上Hard F1约0.65，但最终答案正确率仅约29%。提出的模糊和软注意力指标与最终答案正确性的Spearman相关系数约0.48，优于原始ChainEval指标的0.38-0.46，展现出更强的诊断能力。

Conclusion: RusFinChain填补了俄语金融可验证推理基准的空白，揭示了当前LLM在俄语金融推理中步骤对齐与最终答案正确性之间的显著差距，提出的新评估指标具有更好的诊断能力。数据集、代码和评估框架已开源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了俄语金融领域可验证思维链推理基准RusFinChain，属于评测基准类工作。虽然专注于金融领域和俄语，但核心贡献是构建评测基准和评估指标，与[多模态评测与基准]方向高度匹配。该基准包含多步推理链的自动验证，属于评测协议与自动评估范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on a Russian financial benchmark with step-level reasoning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Russian language benchmark, financial reasoning, chain-of-thought evaluation, fuzzy alignment metrics, low final answer accuracy

Abstract: Multi-step symbolic reasoning is essential for robust financial analysis, yet most benchmarks neglect intermediate reasoning steps. FINCHAIN introduced verifiable Chain-of-Thought (CoT) evaluation but is limited to English. FINESSE-Bench includes a Russian block but relies on multiple-choice questions without step-level supervision. We present RusFinChain, the first Russian-language symbolic benchmark for verifiable CoT reasoning in finance. It spans 17 domains, 172 topics, and comprises 5,280 parameterized examples from executable Python templates, ensuring contamination-free evaluation. Each example includes a gold-standard reasoning chain with intermediate numeric values for automatic verification. We also introduce enhanced metrics: Fuzzy Numeric Alignment and Soft-Attention Alignment. We evaluate 8 open-weight LLMs on a stratified sample, generating 8,100 responses. Results reveal a substantial reasoning gap: models achieve Hard F1 of ~0.65 for step alignment, but only ~29% of final answers are correct. Our fuzzy and soft metrics show stronger correlation with final-answer correctness (Spearman rho approx 0.48) than the original ChainEval (rho approx 0.38-0.46), demonstrating superior diagnostic power. We release dataset, code, and evaluation framework to foster verifiable financial AI for the Russian-speaking community.

</details>


### [80] [EduArt: An educational-level benchmark for evaluating art history knowledge in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02007)
*Gianmarco Spinaci, Lukas Klic, Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EduArt是一个面向艺术史知识与视觉推理的多模态LLM评测基准，包含871道人工出题，涵盖两种语言和七种题型。对12个模型进行评测发现，多项选择准确率接近饱和，但开放完成和错误识别等生成式题型准确率大幅下降，表明艺术史知识与知识运用能力是分离的，单一格式的基准会高估模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用基准测试中LLM得分接近天花板，但这些聚合指标无法揭示模型在单一学科内的行为。已有的艺术史评测依赖合成问题且很少报告题目层面的属性，缺乏一个严谨的、教育级别的艺术史知识基准来评估多模态LLM的真实能力。

Method: 构建EduArt基准，包含871道人工出题（来自意大利中学练习和美国AP艺术史考试），涵盖两种语言和七种题型（多项选择、开放完成、词内填空、错误识别等）。使用经典测试理论（CTT）和逻辑回归分析格式、语言、图像存在和模型对准确率的影响，并在默认仅答案条件和动机条件（要求书面理由）下评估12个模型。

Result: 基准具有良好的心理测量属性（平均区分度0.514，82.3%为良好区分项）。多项选择准确率接近饱和（6个模型），但开放完成题准确率降至23.9%（Claude Opus 4.6），错误识别题降至6.2%（Claude Sonnet 4.6）。格式是准确率的强独立预测因子。动机条件对准确率的影响主要为负向且因模型家族而异。

Conclusion: 艺术史知识与知识运用能力是分离的能力，单一格式的基准会高估模型的实际能力。在艺术史学术研究中负责任地使用多模态LLM需要先绘制这种能力画像，因为实际任务要求生成和操作内容，而非从固定选项中选择。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了EduArt，一个面向艺术史知识与视觉推理的多模态LLM评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: This benchmark focuses on art history, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords, and does not align with the core directions of continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Art history benchmark, multimodal LLM evaluation, format-dependent accuracy, low personal relevance, no alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language models now score near ceiling on general benchmarks, but these aggregate measures reveal little about how models behave within single disciplines. Existing art-focused evaluations rely on synthetic questions and rarely report item-level properties. This paper introduces EduArt, an educational-level benchmark for art-historical knowledge and visual reasoning in multimodal LLMs. EduArt comprises 871 human-authored questions from Italian secondary-school exercises and US Advanced Placement Art History exams, spanning two languages and seven formats from multiple choice to in-text word placement and error identification. Twelve models from six provider families were evaluated under a default answer-only condition and a motivation condition requiring written justification, and characterized using Classical Test Theory and a logistic regression isolating the effects of format, language, image presence, and model. The benchmark showed strong psychometric properties (mean discrimination 0.514, 82.3 percent good discriminators), while multiple-choice accuracy saturated near ceiling for six models, showing recognition formats alone cannot distinguish frontier models. Format was a strong independent predictor of accuracy: models exceeding 94 percent on multiple choice fell to 23.9 percent on open completion (Claude Opus 4.6) and 6.2 percent on error identification (Claude Sonnet 4.6). The motivation condition changed accuracy in a predominantly negative, family-dependent direction. These dissociations indicate that art-historical knowledge and the ability to deploy it are distinct capabilities, and that single-format benchmarks overestimate what models can reliably do. Mapping this capability profile is a precondition for responsible use of multimodal LLMs in art-historical scholarship, where tasks demand producing and manipulating content rather than selecting from fixed options.

</details>


### [81] [Disentangling Pictorial Cue Understanding from Language Bias in VLMs via Depth Ordering Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01503)
*Yiqian Liu, Iuliia Kotseruba, John K. Tsotsos*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过深度排序（depth-ordering）和"找出不同"（odd-one-out）心理物理学任务，系统评估了 VLM 对图像中深度线索的感知能力。作者构建了 O3-D 数据集（37K 图像，147K 问答对），包含真实和合成 3D 场景，并控制单个深度线索的呈现。评估 12 个开源和商业模型发现，所有模型准确率在 47%-56% 之间（低于随机水平），且存在严重的语言偏差。CoT 和 ICL 均未显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测多关注物体识别、视觉问答等任务，但缺乏对底层视觉感知能力（如深度感知）的系统评估。同时，VLM 的性能往往受到语言偏差的干扰，难以区分模型是真正理解了视觉线索还是依赖语言先验。本文旨在通过精心设计的深度排序任务，分离视觉线索理解与语言偏差的影响。

Method: 结合深度排序和 odd-one-out 心理物理学任务：向 VLM 展示多张图像，其中一张图像中的目标物体与其他图像中的相同物体处于不同深度位置，模型需判断该目标更近还是更远。通过控制单个深度线索（如遮挡、相对大小、纹理梯度、阴影等）的呈现，实现细粒度的线索级分析。语言影响通过改变指代表达的清晰度来考察。提出一个新的度量指标量化视觉 vs 语言敏感性。构建 O3-D 数据集，包含 37K 真实和合成图像及 147K 图像-问题对。

Result: 12 个开源和商业模型在 O3-D 上的深度排序准确率在 47%-56% 之间，均低于随机水平（50%）。新提出的度量指标揭示了模型答案中存在强烈的语言偏差。CoT 和 ICL 均未显著改善深度理解性能。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在深度感知方面表现极差，严重依赖语言偏差而非真正的视觉线索理解。静态图像数据本身可能不足以让模型学习深度理解能力，需要更丰富的训练数据或不同的训练范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个新的深度感知评测数据集 O3-D（37K 图像，147K 问答对），用于评估 VLM 的深度排序能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM depth perception evaluation, not aligned with user's priority on generation or continuous models. Signals: VLM depth perception, language bias analysis, O3-D dataset, below-chance performance, not generation-focused

Abstract: In this paper, we study depth perception of vision-language models (VLMs) to isolate the effects of pictorial depth cues and disentangle vision and language influences on model performance. To this end, we combine depth-ordering and odd-one-out psychophysical tasks: the VLMs are presented with images where one object is at different depth relative to other, otherwise identical, objects, and must determine whether the odd-one-out target is closer or farther to the observer. To create stimuli, we generate 2D views from simulated and real 3D scenes while controlling the presence of individual pictorial depth cues, enabling a fine-grained analysis of cue-level contributions. Language effects are examined by varying referring expression clarity. We also introduce a novel metric to quantify vision-vs-language sensitivities. Applying this methodology, we create the Odd-One-Out Depth (O3-D) dataset with 37K real and synthetic images and 147K image-question pairs. Evaluation of 12 open-source and commercial models on O3-D shows under-utilization of depth cues and depth-ordering accuracies between 47% and 56%, with no model above chance level. At the same time, our metric reveals strong linguistic bias in the answers. Neither chain-of-thought (CoT) nor in-context learning (ICL) significantly improves performance, suggesting that static image data alone may be insufficient for depth understanding. All code, the image generation pipeline, and the O3-D dataset are publicly released at .

</details>


### [82] [MedStreamBench: A Time-Aware Benchmark for Streaming and Proactive Medical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01751)
*Yuan Wang, Shujian Gao, Songtao Jiang, Zhengyu Hu, Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MedStreamBench 是一个面向医疗视频的时序感知基准，包含22个医疗数据集和5419个QA实例，支持回顾、当前、未来和主动预警四种时序设置，评估模型在何时回答以及回答什么的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗视频基准只评估模型是否给出正确答案，但忽略了回答时机的重要性。在真实临床场景中，AI系统不仅需要决定预测什么，还需要决定何时回答、推迟判断或主动发出警报，现有基准无法评估这种时序决策能力。

Method: MedStreamBench 整合了22个医疗数据集和5419个QA实例，设计了四种时序设置（回顾、当前、未来、主动预警），限制模型只能访问时间受限的证据窗口，支持单轮和流式评估。在主动监控设置中，模型需要判断是否以及何时触发临床相关警报。评估指标包括答案正确性、响应及时性和证据后稳定性。

Result: 在主流通用和医疗视觉语言模型上的实验表明，模型在离线识别与时序决策之间存在显著差距，在流式和主动预警设置下性能明显下降。

Conclusion: MedStreamBench 揭示了现有模型在时序感知医疗视频理解方面的不足，为未来研究提供了新的评测方向，强调了"何时回答"与"回答什么"同等重要。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MedStreamBench，一个面向医疗视频的时序感知评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准评估模型在回顾、当前、未来和主动预警四种时序设置下的视频理解能力，是典型的多模态评测工作，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical video benchmarks, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical video benchmark, Time-aware evaluation, Proactive monitoring, Low relevance to continuous language models or video generation

Abstract: Existing medical video benchmarks primarily evaluate whether a model produces the correct answer, but rarely assess whether it answers at the right time. In real clinical settings, AI systems must decide not only what to predict, but also when to answer, defer judgment, or proactively raise alerts. This creates a critical gap between benchmark evaluation and deployment requirements. We present MedStreamBench, a benchmark for time-aware medical video understanding. MedStreamBench integrates 22 medical datasets and 5,419 QA instances across four temporal settings: retrospective, present, future, and proactive. Unlike conventional benchmarks that assume full-video access, MedStreamBench restricts models to temporally bounded evidence windows and supports both single-turn and streaming evaluation. We further introduce a proactive monitoring setting that requires models to determine whether and when clinically relevant alerts should be triggered. Beyond answer correctness, MedStreamBench evaluates temporal behavior through responsiveness and post-evidence stability. Experiments on leading general-purpose and medical vision-language models reveal a substantial gap between offline recognition and temporally grounded decision-making, with performance dropping markedly in streaming and proactive settings. Our benchmark is available at .

</details>


### [83] [MMBench-Live: A Continuously Evolving Benchmark for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01813)
*Yuanzhi Liu, Shousheng Zhao, Bo Zhou, Kongming Liang, Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMBench-Live 是一个由多智能体驱动的自动化流水线构建的持续演化多模态评测基准，通过结构化规范、实时数据采集和可验证 QA 生成实现低成本、抗污染的基准更新，并采用分布一致更新策略保持跨版本可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多模态评测基准是静态的，容易随时间过时、遭受数据污染且维护成本高昂，亟需一种可持续演化的基准构建范式。

Method: 提出多智能体驱动的自动化流水线，将基准演化视为任务引导的数据集构建过程，包含结构化基准规范、反馈控制的实时数据采集、带可执行推理的可验证 QA 生成，以及分布一致更新策略（从原基准提取任务相关视觉模式以指导数据收集和过滤）。

Result: 从 MMBench 实例化得到 MMBench-Live，包含 5.9K 新生成的评测实例，答案正确率高，每次更新成本约 30 美元、耗时 1-2 小时。实验表明其保持稳定的模型排名、与原基准语义对齐，且污染相关记忆信号更弱。

Conclusion: MMBench-Live 提供了一种实用且可扩展的可持续多模态基准演化范式，有效解决了静态基准的时效性、污染和维护问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MMBench-Live，一个持续演化的多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该论文与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配，因此直接复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: While the benchmark evolution pipeline is technically sound, it does not directly address the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: benchmark evolution, multi-agent pipeline, data contamination mitigation, distribution-consistent update, low-cost maintenance

Abstract: Evaluation benchmarks are essential for assessing vision-language models (VLMs), but most multimodal benchmarks are static, making them vulnerable to temporal staleness, data contamination, and costly maintenance. We present MMBench-Live, a continuously evolving multimodal benchmark built by a multi-agent-driven automated pipeline. Our framework treats benchmark evolution as task-guided dataset construction, integrating structured benchmark specification, feedback-controlled real-time data acquisition, and verifiable QA generation with executable reasoning. To maintain cross-version comparability, we introduce a distribution-consistent update strategy that extracts task-related visual patterns from the original benchmark to guide data collection and filtering. Instantiated from MMBench, MMBench-Live contains 5.9K newly generated evaluation instances with a high answer correctness rate, while each update costs about USD 30 and takes 1-2 hours. Extensive evaluations show that MMBench-Live preserves stable model rankings, maintains semantic alignment with the original benchmark, and exhibits weaker contamination-related memorization signals, suggesting a practical and scalable paradigm for sustainable multimodal benchmark evolution. The project is available at .

</details>


### [84] [Assessing VLM Reliability for Medical Image Quality Evaluation Under Corruption and Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01973)
*Sofiane Ouaari, Kevin Vorwalder, Nico Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了16个VLM在7种医学图像退化类型和5个严重程度下的零样本质量评估表现，发现像素化导致评分下降最大（平均-20.58%），而亮度影响最小（-0.81%）。文本属性（机构声望、设备年限等）显著影响评分，偏差幅度可达+95.62%和-37.7%，表明当前VLM在医学图像质量评估中存在可靠性和客观性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像质量评估（MIQA）对诊断准确性和患者安全至关重要。自动化MIQA可减轻人工负担，但VLM在真实场景中面对图像退化或文本上下文影响时的行为尚未充分探索，需要系统评估其可靠性。

Method: 使用MediMeta-C数据集，在零样本设置下对16个VLM、7种模态进行7种退化类型（像素化、模糊、噪声等）和5个严重程度的评测。分析嵌入几何变化与评分的关系，并测试文本属性（人口统计、专业知识、基础设施、机构）对评分的影响。

Result: 像素化导致最大评分下降（平均-20.58%，OCT上达-34.4%），亮度影响最小（-0.81%）。嵌入位移与评分变化相关。同族模型相关性0.67-0.83，部分模型对退化乳腺X光图像评分反而上升+31%。文本属性中，机构声望提升评分+17.15%，设备年限降低评分-14.7%。最大变化为InternVL-8B的+95.62%和MedGemma的-37.7%。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在医学图像质量评估中存在显著局限性：像素化（一种隐私保护变换）会降低性能，表明患者隐私与可靠性之间存在权衡；对上下文元数据的敏感性表明客观性不足，元数据本身也是隐私和偏差来源。隐私保护和客观质量评估是部署前需要同时满足的要求。

Classification reason: 该论文对VLM在医学图像质量评估中的可靠性进行系统性评测，涉及多种退化类型、严重程度和文本属性偏差，属于多模态评测与基准方向。虽然聚焦医学领域，但核心是评测VLM的鲁棒性和偏差，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM reliability, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical image quality, VLM robustness, corruption sensitivity, textual bias, privacy trade-off

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied in medical tasks such as pathology description, report generation, and visual question answering. Medical Image Quality Assessment (MIQA) supports diagnostic accuracy and patient safety by determining whether images meet the standards required for clinical decision-making. Automating MIQA with VLMs may reduce workload, but their behavior under real-world conditions, where images may be degraded or textual context may affect judgments, should be further explored before deployment. We benchmark VLMs on medical image quality using the MediMeta-C dataset zero-shot across seven corruption types and five severity levels. We evaluate sensitivity to degradation patterns, the effect of corruptions on embedding geometry, and whether textual attributes (demographics, expertise, infrastructure, institution) alter scores. Across 16 VLMs and seven modalities, pixelation produced the largest score reductions (mean -20.58%, up to -34.4% for OCT), whereas brightness had limited effect (-0.81%). Embedding displacement was associated with score changes. Same-family models showed correlations of 0.67-0.83; some produced increases up to +31% for corrupted mammography. Textual attributes affected scores: institutional prestige raised them +17.15%, and equipment age lowered them -14.7%. The largest changes were +95.62% (InternVL-8B) and -37.7% (MedGemma). Current VLMs show limitations for medical image quality assessment. Pixelation, a privacy-preserving transformation, reduces performance, indicating a trade-off between patient privacy and reliability. Sensitivity to contextual metadata indicates limited objectivity and marks metadata as a privacy and bias source. Privacy protection and objective quality assessment are related requirements for use.

</details>


### [85] [LongEgoRefer: A Benchmark for Long-Form Egocentric Video Referring Expression Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02096)
*Shunya Kato, Taiki Miyanishi, Shuhei Kurita, Mahiro Ukai, Nakamasa Inoue, Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LongEgoRefer 是一个基于 Ego4D 长视频构建的自我中心视频指代理解基准，包含 1,498 条指代表达，平均视频时长 45 分钟，具有目标稀疏、描述详细、交互复杂等特点。现有方法在该基准上表现不佳，揭示了长视频时空指代定位的固有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心视频指代理解（Video REC）基准主要关注短视频片段，目标物体在帧中密集出现，无法反映真实世界中长视频（未裁剪、目标稀疏、活动转换复杂）的场景。因此需要构建一个更具挑战性的长视频基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 从 Ego4D 数据集中选取长视频，构建包含 1,498 条指代表达的 LongEgoRefer 基准。每条指代表达需要模型在长达 45 分钟的视频中定位目标物体的时空范围。评估了多种现有 Video REC 方法，包括基于视觉语言模型结合 Grounded SAM2 的无训练基线方法。

Result: 现有先进模型和基线方法在 LongEgoRefer 上表现显著下降，揭示了长视频自我中心时空指代定位的固有难度。具体结果展示了目标稀疏性和长视频时长对模型性能的严重影响。

Conclusion: LongEgoRefer 是一个具有挑战性的长视频自我中心指代理解基准，现有方法难以应对其目标稀疏、长时序和复杂交互的特点，强调了需要更鲁棒的视频理解模型来推动该任务的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向长视频的第一人称视角指代理解基准（LongEgoRefer），属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准聚焦于视频指代理解（Video REC）任务，与[多模态评测与基准]（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides a challenging long-form egocentric benchmark exposing limitations of current Video REC models, but is a benchmark paper not directly advancing core priority subtopics. Signals: long-form egocentric video, benchmark, sparse target, spatio-temporal grounding, Ego4D

Abstract: Egocentric videos capture rich and diverse human-object interactions and have emerged as a fundamental resource for understanding human activities related to objects. In this context, Video Referring Expression Comprehension (Video REC), the task of localizing the temporal and spatial extent of a referred object in video frames given a natural language query, plays a key role in linking textual descriptions to observed objects in untrimmed egocentric recordings. However, existing egocentric Video REC benchmarks primarily focus on short video clips, where some target object appears densely within frames. Such settings do not reflect real-world egocentric recordings, which are long-form, untrimmed, and characterized by sparse object occurrences and complex activity transitions. To address this limitation, we introduce LongEgoRefer, a novel and challenging benchmark constructed from long-form videos in the Ego4D dataset. LongEgoRefer contains 1,498 referring expressions with an average video duration of 45 minutes. The benchmark exhibits extreme target sparsity, detailed linguistic descriptions, and complex human-object interactions embedded in long, dynamic egocentric narratives. Consequently, it defines a demanding spatio-temporal grounding problem that requires models to identify both when an event occurs and where the referred object appears within extended video sequences. We evaluate existing Video REC approaches, including training-free baselines based on vision-language models combined with Grounded SAM2. Extensive experiments show that even advanced baselines and current state-of-the-art models struggle significantly on LongEgoRefer. These results highlight the intrinsic difficulty of long-form egocentric spatio-temporal grounding and emphasize the need for more robust video understanding models.

</details>


### [86] [AnyGroundBench: A Specialized-Domain Benchmark for Video Grounding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02269)
*Rintaro Otsubo, Ryo Fujii, Reina Ishikawa, Taiki Kanaya, Kanta Sawafuji, Hiroki Kajita, Shigeki Sakai, Hideo Saito, Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

TL;DR: AnyGroundBench 是一个面向专业领域的视频时空定位（STVG）领域自适应评测基准，覆盖动物行为、工业、体育、手术和公共安全五个专业领域，提供密集的时空标注和专用训练子集。对15个SOTA VLM的评估表明，当前模型在专业领域的零样本和上下文学习适应中均表现不佳，暴露了时空推理能力的严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 STVG 评测仅局限于日常场景的零样本评估，与专业领域（如动物行为分析、工业监控、手术视频等）的实际应用存在严重脱节。由于无法对所有数据分布进行预训练，模型对新领域的适应能力至关重要，但目前缺乏相应的评测基准。

Method: 构建 AnyGroundBench 基准，包含五个专业领域（动物、工业、体育、手术、公共安全），将新拍摄的视频（如专家标注的小鼠行为视频）与已有数据集统一，提供密集高保真时空标注，并设置专用训练子集以系统衡量领域自适应能力。评估15个SOTA VLM的零样本泛化和上下文学习能力。

Result: 当前 VLM 在专业领域的零样本和 ICL 自适应中均表现不佳，暴露出时空推理能力的严重缺陷。论文通过实验揭示了现有模型在专业领域泛化上的显著不足。

Conclusion: AnyGroundBench 将 STVG 评测范式从静态零样本测试转向严格的领域自适应评估，揭示了当前 VLM 在专业领域时空推理中的关键缺陷，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 AnyGroundBench，一个面向视频时空定位（STVG）的领域自适应评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向。该基准覆盖动物、工业、体育、手术、公共安全五个专业领域，评估 VLM 的零样本泛化与上下文学习能力。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Benchmark for specialized video grounding, but not aligned with user's priority on continuous models or generation. Signals: video grounding benchmark, domain adaptation, spatio-temporal reasoning, VLM evaluation, specialized domains

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated immense promise in Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG). However, current evaluation protocols are largely confined to zero-shot assessments on general, daily-life benchmarks. This creates a critical disconnect from real-world applications in specialized fields, where models inevitably encounter rare visual concepts and complex spatio-temporal dynamics. Since exhaustive pre-training across infinite data distributions is infeasible, the ability to adapt to novel domains is essential. To bridge this gap, we introduce AnyGroundBench, a domain-adaptation benchmark designed to shift the STVG evaluation paradigm from static zero-shot testing to rigorous domain adaptation. Targeting five specialized domains (animal, industry, sports, surgery, and public security), AnyGroundBench pairs newly captured videos such as expert-annotated mouse behaviors with established datasets, unifying them through dense, high-fidelity spatio-temporal annotations. Crucially, the benchmark provides dedicated training subsets to systematically measure domain adaptability. We extensively evaluate 15 state-of-the-art VLMs, assessing their zero-shot generalization and In-Context Learning (ICL) capabilities under practical computational constraints. Ultimately, our findings reveal that current models fail in both zero-shot and ICL-based adaptation when confronted with specialized domains, exposing critical flaws in spatio-temporal reasoning that future research must address.

</details>

### [87] [SAB-LVLM: Significance-Aware Binarization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01876)
*Qi Lyu, Jiahua Dong, Baichen Liu, Xudong Wang, Mingfei Han, Yulun Zhang, Fahad Shahbaz Khan, Salman Khan, Lianqing Liu, Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (77.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SAB-LVLM 提出了一种显著性感知的二值化方法，通过构建 Hessian 矩阵和空间显著性图来区分不同层和模态下权重的重要性，并设计模态引导的集成策略生成二值化映射，在约1-bit压缩下显著优于现有二值化PTQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM参数量巨大，跨模态计算导致高内存和延迟，难以部署在资源受限设备上。已有的二值化方法忽略了不同层和模态间权重重要性的差异，导致与下游任务无关的参数被不必要保留，而模态关键权重未能充分优化，造成性能严重下降。

Method: 首先为文本和视觉输入构建Hessian矩阵，提出空间显著性图来区分单模态激活权重和跨模态激活权重；然后设计模态引导的集成策略获得显著性感知的二值化映射，衡量各层和各模态的权重重要性；最后将该映射作为误差重加权项融入二值化目标，通过交替显著性加权更新方案进行二值化拟合。

Result: 在多个LVLM和基准上的大量实验表明，SAB-LVLM在约1-bit压缩约束下显著优于现有的二值化PTQ方法。

Conclusion: SAB-LVLM通过显著性感知的二值化方法有效解决了LVLM压缩中权重重要性差异被忽视的问题，在极低比特量化下实现了更好的性能-效率权衡，为LVLM在资源受限设备上的部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的二值化压缩方法，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel binarization for LVLMs with significance awareness, but focuses on model compression rather than core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: binarization, large vision-language model, model compression, Hessian matrix, significance-aware

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in multimodal understanding, yet their enormous parameter scale and cross-modal computation incur substantial memory and latency overhead, severely limiting real-world deployment on resource-constrained devices. Binarization offers an attractive solution by drastically reducing storage and computational costs. However, existing binarization methods neglect the varying importance of weights across different layers and modalities. This causes parameters irrelevant to downstream tasks to be unnecessarily retained, whereas modality-critical weights may not be adequately optimized, resulting in significant performance degradation. To address these challenges, we develop a novel \underline{S}ignificance-\underline{A}ware \underline{B}inarization for \underline{L}arge \underline{V}ision-\underline{L}anguage \underline{M}odels (SAB-LVLM). Specifically, after constructing Hessian matrices for textual and visual inputs, we propose a spatial significance map to distinguish full-precision weights activated under a single modality from those activated across modalities. We then devise a modality-guided integration strategy to obtain the significance-aware binarization map, which measures weight significance across layers and modalities. Subsequently, this binarization map is incorporated into the binarization objective as an error reweighting term, and binarization fitting is performed through an alternating significance-weighted update scheme. Extensive experiments illustrate the superiority of our SAB-LVLM over existing binary PTQ methods under an approximately 1-bit compression constraint. Our code is accessible at .

</details>


### [88] [Efficient PEFT Methods with Adaptive Checkpointing for Vision Models and VLMs on Resource Constrained Consumer-GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02158)
*Altay Toktassyn, Jurn-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种PEFT方法（Full FT、LoRA、AdaLoRA、QLoRA、BitFit）在Transformer和Mamba视觉骨干上的表现，并提出了一种内存预算感知的自适应梯度检查点算法。实验表明QLoRA和BitFit在1-2%精度损失下可降低20-30%能耗，自适应算法减少43-79%峰值内存但带来9-30%能耗开销；DINOv2零样本评估在CIFAR-100上超越微调模型，而小型自回归VLM竞争力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预训练视觉模型在微调时需要大量GPU内存，难以部署在边缘设备上。现有PEFT方法虽能减少参数量，但训练过程中的中间激活仍会占用大量显存。本文旨在系统比较不同PEFT方法在资源受限消费级GPU上的表现，并提出一种自适应梯度检查点策略以进一步降低内存峰值。

Method: 1) 比较五种PEFT方法：Full FT、LoRA、AdaLoRA、QLoRA、BitFit；2) 在Transformer（ViT-Small、TinyViT）和Mamba（Vim-Small、MambaVision-T）视觉骨干上评估；3) 提出一种内存预算感知的自适应梯度检查点算法，根据可用VRAM动态决定哪些层需要检查点；4) 评估三类基础模型基线：零样本对比VLM（OpenCLIP、SigLIP）、自监督视觉骨干+DINOv2轻量评估、自回归VLM（PaliGemma、MobileVLM、SmolVLM）；5) 使用CIFAR-100和DTD数据集，报告精度、训练时间、能耗及扩展的NetScore多目标指标。

Result: QLoRA和BitFit在1-2%精度损失下降低20-30%能耗；自适应梯度检查点算法减少43-79%峰值内存但带来9-30%能耗开销；DINOv2在CIFAR-100上以0.917精度超越微调模型（0.897），且能耗极低；小型自回归VLM在分类任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 在资源受限场景下，QLoRA和BitFit是精度与效率的最佳折中方案；自适应检查点算法能有效降低内存峰值，适合2GB VRAM等极端受限环境；DINOv2结合轻量评估协议是低资源视觉分类的高效替代方案；小型自回归VLM在分类任务上尚不具备竞争力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究在资源受限GPU上对视觉模型和VLM进行参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，包括LoRA、AdaLoRA、QLoRA、BitFit等，并提出了自适应梯度检查点策略以减少峰值内存。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）的典型范畴，该[CANONICAL] subtopic明确包含PEFT、量化、高效推理等关键词。

Importance reason: Focuses on efficient fine-tuning for vision models, but lacks direct connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: PEFT comparison on consumer GPUs, adaptive checkpointing reduces memory, QLoRA/BitFit energy efficient, DINOv2 zero-shot strong, small autoregressive VLMs weak

Abstract: Modern pretrained vision models achieve strong accuracy but demand substantial GPU memory for fine-tuning, making edge deployment impractical. This paper compares five parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods (Full FT, LoRA, AdaLoRA, QLoRA, BitFit) on Transformers- (ViT-Small, TinyViT) and Mamba-based vision backbones (Vim-Small, MambaVision-T) under an on-device VRAM budget (e.g., 2 GB), together with three gradient-checkpointing strategies (none, static, and a proposed memory-budget-aware adaptive algorithm); and we evaluate three families of foundation-model baselines: zero-shot contrastive vision language models (OpenCLIP, SigLIP), self-supervised vision backbones with lightweight evaluation protocols (DINOv2), and autoregressive VLMs for prompt-based classification (PaliGemma, MobileVLM, SmolVLM). Experiments on CIFAR-100 and DTD report accuracy, training time, energy, and the NetScore family of multi-objective metrics, which we extend with two deployment-aware variants. QLoRA and BitFit cut energy 20-30% at a 1-2% accuracy cost; the adaptive algorithm reduces peak memory 43-79% with 9-30% energy overhead. DINOv2 surpasses fine-tuned models on CIFAR-100 (0.917 vs. 0.897) at a fraction of the energy, while small autoregressive VLMs remain uncompetitive.

</details>


### [89] [When Token Compression Breaks: Structural Pruning vs. Token Reduction for Robust ViT Segmentation under High Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02237)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen, Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (71.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了ViT语义分割中token压缩与结构剪枝两种高效化方法在高压缩率下的表现，发现token压缩在轻度压缩时有效但高压缩下性能急剧下降，而结构剪枝退化更平滑。结合剪枝后适度token压缩的策略能在高压缩下取得更好的精度-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT语义分割的token压缩方法评估主要关注低到中等压缩率，缺乏对高压缩率和损坏输入下行为的理解；同时结构剪枝作为另一种高效化路径很少与token压缩在统一协议下比较。

Method: 在ADE20K/Cityscapes及其损坏变体上，以匹配FLOPs为条件，系统对比了代表性token压缩方法（如Token-Learn、Dynamic Token Pruning等）与结构剪枝方法（如结构化剪枝ViT），并提出了"先剪枝后合并"（prune-then-merge）的组合策略。

Result: token压缩在轻度压缩下效果好但高压缩时性能急剧下降；结构剪枝退化曲线更平滑，在高压缩下更稳定；组合策略在同等FLOPs下实现了更好的精度-鲁棒性权衡。

Conclusion: 对于部署导向的ViT语义分割，结构剪枝在高压缩场景下优于token压缩，而先剪枝后适度token压缩的组合策略是更优的实践方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究ViT语义分割中的token压缩与结构剪枝方法，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴，关注模型效率优化与鲁棒性权衡。

Importance reason: Provides systematic comparison of token compression vs. structural pruning for ViT segmentation under high compression, but focuses on robustness rather than the user's priority subtopics. Signals: Token compression vs. structural pruning, high compression robustness, ViT segmentation, ADE20K-C and Cityscapes-C, prune-then-merge strategy

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are strong backbones for semantic segmentation, but their computational cost limits deployment. Recent token compression methods for efficient transformer-based segmentation reduce this cost by decreasing the number of tokens. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on low-to-moderate compression, leaving their behavior under aggressive compression and corrupted inputs unclear. Meanwhile, structural pruning provides an orthogonal route to efficiency by removing redundant components in the ViT architecture, but is rarely compared to token compression under a unified protocol. To bridge this gap, we benchmark representative token compression and structural pruning methods for ViT-based semantic segmentation under matched FLOPs on ADE20K and Cityscapes, together with their common-corruption variants ADE20K-C and Cityscapes-C. Our results reveal a consistent trend on both clean and corrupted inputs: token compression is highly effective at mild reductions but degrades sharply when compression becomes severe, consistent with substantial information loss from overly aggressive token reduction. In contrast, structural pruning exhibits a smoother degradation curve and is more stable at high compression. Motivated by these findings, we study a prune-then-merge pipeline that applies moderate token compression on top of a moderately pruned backbone. At comparable FLOPs, this combined strategy consistently achieves a better accuracy-robustness trade-off at high compression, offering a practical recipe for deployment-oriented ViT segmentation. Code is available at .

</details>


### [90] [InduceKV: Fixed-Footprint Continual Adaptation of Multimodal LLMs via Inducing KV Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02010)
*Qianyu Chen, Ziteng Feng, Canran Xiao, Runxuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: InduceKV 提出一种基于检索的固定内存持续适配方法，通过双层选择构建紧凑的诱导集，将训练前缀存储为注意力就绪的 KV 记忆条目，在固定内存预算下实现多模态 LLM 的持续学习，在多项持续学习任务上超越 PEFT、MoE、回放和提示检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型需要持续适应新任务和领域，但传统方法（参数更新或回放存储）会随着时间累积大量适配状态，导致部署内存不断增长。如何在固定内存预算下实现持续适配是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出 InduceKV，一种检索式方法。它将每个选中的训练前缀存储为注意力就绪的记忆条目，包含冻结的检索键和紧凑的逐层 KV 载荷，可附加到模型的自注意力缓存中。通过双层选择构建紧凑诱导集：轻量级校准适配检索，同时所选记忆平衡当前任务似然、基于锚点的保留和冻结检索空间中的覆盖度。

Result: 在任务增量指令微调、持续 VQA、领域增量适配和终身多模态指令微调等设置下，InduceKV 在匹配内存预算的条件下持续优于 PEFT、MoE、回放和提示检索基线。额外诊断实验表明增益并非来自更强的骨干网络、单纯回放或无限候选池。

Conclusion: InduceKV 通过检索式 KV 记忆诱导实现了固定内存预算下的多模态 LLM 持续适配，为部署场景下的高效增量学习提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大语言模型在固定内存预算下的持续适配方法，通过检索式KV记忆压缩实现高效增量学习，属于多模态模型高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient continual adaptation for multimodal LLMs under fixed memory, aligning with user's model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: Fixed-footprint continual adaptation, Retrieval-based KV memory, Bilevel selection for compact inducing set, Outperforms PEFT, MoE, replay baselines, Strong match to efficient inference and MLLM subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal large language models must adapt to evolving tasks and domains, yet continual improvement under bounded deployment footprint remains difficult because repeated parameter updates or growing replay stores can accumulate adaptation state over time. We study fixed-footprint continual adaptation: the deployed adaptation state is kept under a fixed memory budget, while the backbone model is left unchanged and task-specific updates are externalized. We propose InduceKV, a retrieval-based method that stores each selected training prefix as an attention-ready memory entry, consisting of a frozen retrieval key and compact layerwise key--value (KV) payloads that can be appended to the model's self-attention cache. Under a strict memory budget, InduceKV constructs a compact inducing set through bilevel selection: a lightweight calibration is fit for retrieval, while the selected memory balances current-task likelihood, anchor-based retention, and coverage in the frozen retrieval space. Across task-incremental instruction tuning, continual VQA, domain-incremental adaptation, and lifelong multimodal instruction tuning, InduceKV consistently improves over PEFT, MoE, replay, and prompt-retrieval baselines under matched memory budgets. We further report backbone-matched, stage-1 CoIN, compute-matched, and scalability diagnostics, showing that the gains are not due to a stronger backbone, replay alone, or an unbounded candidate pool.

</details>


### [91] [Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02484)
*Xuehui Wang, Xuankun Yang, Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (81.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP)，一种基于熵感知的密集视觉 token 剪枝框架，通过统计熵过滤文本噪声并将 token 选择建模为子模最大化问题，在严格 token 预算下保留细粒度视觉线索，提升 VLM 的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉 token 剪枝方法在密集指令和细粒度查询场景下难以保留关键视觉线索，原因是文本噪声污染了跨模态评分，且标准 token 选择导致特征碎片化。

Method: EADP 框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 利用统计熵量化并过滤文本噪声，得到鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性分数；(2) 将 token 选择建模为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，确保整体且非冗余的视觉表征。

Result: 大量实验表明，EADP 在严格 token 预算下鲁棒地保留了细粒度视觉线索，在多个具有挑战性的多模态基准上取得了最先进的性能，改善了 VLM 的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: EADP 通过熵感知去噪和子模选择两个关键设计，有效解决了密集指令下视觉 token 剪枝的瓶颈问题，为 VLM 的高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉 token 剪枝以加速 VLM，属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化"（efficient_multimodal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient VLM inference via visual token pruning, aligning with model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: visual token pruning, entropy-aware, submodular maximization, VLM acceleration, fine-grained visual cues

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.

</details>
