### [92] [AIriskEval-edu: New Dataset for Risk Assessment in AI-mediated K-12 Educational Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01934)
*Javier Irigoyen, Roberto Daza, Francisco Jurado, Julian Fierrez, Ruben Tolosana, Alvaro Ortigosa, Enrique Blas, Aythami Morales*

Main category: cs.DB, cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了AIriskEval-edu-db2数据集，包含1,639条K-12教学解释（来自170个ScienceQA问题），涵盖人类教师编写的解释和LLM模拟不同教师画像生成的11种解释，并针对事实准确性、深度完整性、焦点相关性、学生适龄性和意识形态偏见五个维度进行风险评估。数据集还包含785条带有结构化可解释性注释（风险定位与描述）的样本。实验验证了在本地部署的Llama 3.1 8B模型上微调后，可在教育审计任务中接近或超越前沿商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12教育中LLM生成的教学内容可能存在多种风险（如事实错误、不适当内容、意识形态偏见等），但目前缺乏专门针对教育场景的LLM生成内容风险评估数据集和审计工具，且现有方案依赖商业API存在隐私问题。

Method: 从ScienceQA中选取170个K-12问题，为每个问题收集1条人类教师解释和11条LLM模拟不同教师画像生成的解释（共1,639条）。设计包含5个维度（事实精度、深度完整性、焦点相关性、学生适龄性、意识形态偏见）的风险评估标准。通过半自动流程+专家教师验证，为785条解释添加结构化可解释性注释（风险定位+描述）。使用该数据集对Llama 3.1 8B进行监督微调，并与GPT-4o等商业模型对比。

Result: 实验表明，在AIriskEval-edu-db2上微调后的Llama 3.1 8B在风险检测和可解释性评估任务上能够接近甚至超越GPT-4o等前沿商业模型，同时保持本地部署的隐私优势。

Conclusion: AIriskEval-edu-db2数据集为教育场景下LLM生成内容的风险评估提供了标准化基准，证明了轻量级本地模型通过微调可以在教育审计任务中达到有竞争力的性能，为保护学生隐私的教育AI审计提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个用于K-12教育场景下LLM生成教学解释的风险评估数据集（AIriskEval-edu），包含人工标注的风险维度、可解释性注释等，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测与数据集构建工作。该论文与multimodal_understanding_generation方向下的generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）子主题高度匹配，因为其核心贡献是构建了一个新的评测数据集并进行了模型验证实验。

Importance reason: Dataset and risk assessment for educational AI explanations, but not aligned with user's priority on generative models and continuous representations. Signals: K-12 educational dataset, LLM-based risk auditing, ScienceQA explanations, pedagogical risk rubric, fine-tuning Llama 3.1 8B

Abstract: This work introduces AIriskEval-edu-db2, a new dataset designed to train and evaluate auditors based on LLMs for an explainable pedagogical risk assessment in instructional content for grades K-12. The dataset comprises 1,639 explanations from 170 curated ScienceQA questions, covering science, language arts, and social sciences. For each question, the dataset includes an explanation written by a human teacher alongside 11 explanations generated by LLM-simulated teacher profiles associated with distinct pedagogical risks. We propose a comprehensive risk rubric aligned with established educational standards that covers five complementary dimensions: factual precision, depth and completeness, focus and relevance, student-level appropriateness, and ideological bias. A key contribution is the addition of 785 explanations with structured explainability annotations, including risk localization and risk description. The annotations are produced through a semi-automatic process with expert teacher validation. Finally, we present validation experiments comparing state-of-the-art proprietary models with a lightweight local Llama 3.1 8B model in both the pedagogical risk detection and the explainability assessment. These experiments evaluate whether supervised fine-tuning on AIriskEval-edu-db2 enables a locally deployable model to approach or outperform stronger frontier models while preserving privacy in educational auditing and assessment tasks.

</details>

### [93] [Bridging 3D Gaussians and Semantic Occupancy for Comprehensive Open-Vocabulary Scene Understanding from Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01633)
*Hu Zhu, Bohan Li, Xianda Guo, Yanlun Peng, Zheng Zhu, Xin Jin, Wenjun Zeng, Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: COVScene提出一种无位姿语义高斯框架，将可渲染的3D高斯原语与密集语义占据场通过可微体积提升耦合，支持新视角合成、开放词汇语义查询和语义占据预测，无需外部相机标定或体素级监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈高斯方法在无位姿场景下重建和语义渲染方面取得进展，但高斯原语主要通过图像空间目标优化，在未观测区域约束弱，难以支持完整的3D场景理解（如语义占据预测）。

Method: 提出COVScene框架：1）语义感知的Geometry Transformer从稀疏无位姿图像预测高斯参数；2）多任务高斯解码器输出RGB、语义特征和占据信息；3）通过可微体积提升将语义高斯投影到密集占据场，在训练计算图中施加体积正则化；4）结合几何基础模型蒸馏和占据熵正则化。

Result: 在ScanNet和ScanNet++上，COVScene保持竞争性渲染质量，提升开放词汇分割性能，并在无直接体素监督下实现更强的语义占据预测。

Conclusion: 将可渲染高斯与语义占据场在训练时耦合，能有效提升无位姿场景下的3D开放词汇理解能力，为统一场景表示提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文从无位姿稀疏图像进行开放词汇3D场景理解，涉及3D高斯与语义占据场的结合，属于空间3D推理与多模态理解的交叉方向。最佳匹配方向为多模态理解与生成下的[空间与3D多模态推理]（spatial_3d_reasoning），该子主题涵盖3D视觉定位、几何推理等。同时论文也涉及3D高斯渲染（多模态生成下的3D生成与重建），但核心贡献在场景理解与语义推理。

Importance reason: Strong technical contribution in 3D scene understanding but only tangential to user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian splatting, open-vocabulary scene understanding, unposed images, semantic occupancy, differentiable volumetric lifting

Abstract: Comprehensive 3D scene understanding from sparse, unposed images requires a model to recover renderable geometry, open-vocabulary semantics, and free/occupied 3D space without relying on external camera calibration. Recent feed-forward Gaussian methods improve pose-free reconstruction and semantic rendering, but their Gaussian primitives are mainly optimized through image-space objectives and remain weakly constrained in unobserved regions. We propose \textit{COVScene}, a pose-free semantic Gaussian framework that couples renderable Gaussian primitives with a dense semantic occupancy field through differentiable volumetric lifting. Instead of converting Gaussians to voxels only at evaluation time, COVScene lifts the predicted semantic Gaussians inside the training computation graph, so volumetric regularization provides gradients to Gaussian opacity, geometry, and semantic features. The framework combines a semantic-aware Geometry Transformer, multi-task Gaussian decoding, geometric foundation distillation, and occupancy entropy regularization to support novel view synthesis, open-vocabulary semantic querying, and semantic occupancy prediction within a single representation. Experiments on ScanNet and ScanNet++ show that COVScene maintains competitive rendering quality, improves open-vocabulary segmentation, and achieves stronger semantic occupancy prediction than the self-supervised baseline without direct voxel-level supervision.

</details>


### [94] [SpaceEra++: A Unified Framework Towards 3D Spatial Reasoning in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01784)
*Weili Guan, Haoyu Zhang, Meng Liu, Qianlong Xiang, Yaowei Wang, Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpaceEra++ 是一个面向视频中3D空间推理的统一框架，通过ScenePick帧采样策略和SpaceAlign空间对齐方法，缓解了VLM在2D观测下的空间不确定性和数据稀缺问题，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLM受限于2D观测带来的空间不确定性以及3D空间理解数据的稀缺性，难以有效进行视觉空间推理。现有方法在扫描视频输入不足和推理约束较弱方面存在局限。

Method: 提出SpaceEra++框架，包含：(1) ScenePick帧采样策略，平衡空间覆盖与物体语义，生成紧凑而全面的场景表示；(2) SpaceAlign对齐方法，联合利用绝对坐标和相对空间关系强制执行成对物体约束，使优化与空间准确性对齐。

Result: 在多个基准上相比强基线取得一致提升，消融实验验证了各组件的单独和联合贡献。

Conclusion: SpaceEra++通过数据构建、模型设计、训练优化和提示推理的全流程改进，有效增强了VLM的3D空间推理能力，并为未来研究提供了指导。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于视频中的3D空间推理，旨在增强VLM的空间理解能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题。

Importance reason: Advances 3D spatial reasoning in video for VLMs, but lacks direct connection to your priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: 3D spatial reasoning, video understanding, VLM improvement, frame sampling strategy, spatial alignment

Abstract: Visual-spatial understanding, defined as the ability to infer object relationships and scene layouts from visual inputs, is fundamental to downstream tasks such as robotic navigation and embodied interaction. However, pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) remain constrained by spatial uncertainty stemming from inherently 2D observations and by the scarcity of data for 3D spatial understanding. To address these limitations, we proposed a novel framework, SpaceEra, in the NeurIPS 2025 Spotlight paper. Although it achieved significant performance gains, we further observed that its effectiveness is hindered by insufficient input from scanning videos and weak reasoning constraints. To tackle these newly emerged challenges, we extend the original framework into a comprehensive system, termed SpaceEra++, which spans data construction, model design, training optimization, and prompting inference. Specifically, to alleviate input insufficiency, we introduce ScenePick, a frame sampling strategy that balances spatial coverage with object semantics to produce compact yet comprehensive scene representations. In addition, to enhance spatial reasoning, we develop SpaceAlign, which enforces pairwise object constraints by jointly exploiting absolute coordinates and relative spatial relations, thereby aligning optimization with spatial accuracy. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, while ablation studies validate both the individual and joint contributions of each component, and further analyses provide guidance for future research.

</details>


### [95] [Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02497)
*Song Tang, Shuming Hu, Xincheng Shuai, Henghui Ding, Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出主动全景指代分割（APRS）任务及 PanoSeeker 方法，将 VLM 与显式空间视觉记忆 EgoSphere 结合，通过监督微调与强化学习训练，使智能体在 360° 环境中高效探索并分割指令指定的目标物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代分割模型只能处理固定视角的静态图像，无法应用于具身智能中需要在连续 360° 环境中进行主动感知的场景。

Method: 提出 PanoSeeker，集成 VLM 与 EgoSphere（显式空间视觉记忆），将局部观测逐步整合为统一 360° 表征，规划高效无冗余搜索轨迹；构建专家标注搜索轨迹数据集进行 SFT，再通过 RL 后训练优化探索效率。

Result: 在建立的 APRS 基准上，PanoSeeker 在搜索效率和分割精度上显著优于适配的 SOTA 基线方法。

Conclusion: 主动感知与空间记忆结合能有效解决全景环境中的指代分割问题，为具身智能体的主动视觉感知提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出主动全景指代分割（APRS）任务，涉及具身智能体在360°环境中通过调整视角主动探索并分割目标物体，属于空间推理与具身推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 空间与 3D 多模态推理 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel active perception task for panoramic segmentation, but tangential to user's core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and generation. Signals: Active perception, Panoramic segmentation, VLM with spatial memory, Reinforcement learning for exploration, Embodied AI

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($\Delta\theta, \Delta\phi$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>

### [96] [An Efficient vLLM-Based Inference Pipeline for Unified Audio Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02119)
*Haoran Wang, Jinchuan Tian, Siddhant Arora, Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cs.AI, eess.AS

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (87.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 vLLM 的高效推理管线，用于统一的语音理解与生成。通过扩展自回归解码以原生支持延迟模式解交织和多流采样，并在 GPU 上集成声学解码器实现端到端波形合成。特别地，通过连续批处理中协同调度条件/无条件请求，CFG 实现可维持非 CFG 吞吐量的 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型推理引擎（如 vLLM）虽然在高吞吐量理解任务上表现优异，但缺乏对多模态生成的原生支持。这一问题在语音语言模型中尤为严重，因为生成多层音频 token 需要解耦的 AR+NAR 或同步多 token 预测（MTP）与延迟模式交织，这与标准单流自回归循环冲突。

Method: 扩展 vLLM 的自回归解码以原生支持延迟模式解交织和协调多流采样；在 GPU 上集成声学解码器实现端到端波形合成；通过连续批处理中协同调度条件与无条件请求，实现高效的 Classifier-Free Guidance（CFG），避免吞吐量减半。

Result: CFG 实现可维持非 CFG 吞吐量的 80%，有效吸收了双请求和 logit 合并的开销。框架已开源。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精心设计的推理管线优化，可以在不牺牲吞吐量的前提下，为统一语音理解与生成模型提供高效的 CFG 支持，为多模态生成模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于 vLLM 的推理管线，实现统一的语音理解与生成（audio understanding and generation），属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型方向。该方向在 multimodal_understanding_generation 下有现存的非 canonical 子主题 "统一多模态模型"（id: unified_multimodal_model），且论文内容高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for unified multimodal models, a priority subtopic, with a practical vLLM pipeline that maintains CFG throughput. Signals: efficient inference, unified multimodal model, vLLM inference pipeline, CFG throughput optimization, speech language model

Abstract: While Large Multimodal Models excel in comprehension, high-throughput inference engines lack native support for multimodal generation. This is severe in Speech Language Models, where generating multi-layered audio tokens via decoupled AR+NAR or synchronous Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) with delay-pattern interleaving conflicts with standard single-stream loops. We present a vLLM-based inference pipeline for unified speech understanding and generation. We extend autoregressive decoding to natively execute delay-pattern de-interleaving and coordinated multi-stream sampling, integrating an on-GPU acoustic decoder for end-to-end waveform synthesis. Crucially, we overcome the shared intuition that Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) halves throughput. By co-scheduling paired conditional and unconditional requests within a continuous batch, our CFG implementation sustains 80% of non-CFG throughput, absorbing dual-request and logit merging overheads. We open-source our framework.

</details>

### [97] [LLM-Empowered Multimodal Fusion Framework for Autonomous Driving: Semantic Enhancement and Channel-Adaptive Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01772)
*Wen Wang, Yaping Sun, Yejun He, Hao Chen, Zhiyong Chen, Xiaodong Xu, Nan Ma, Shuguang Cui*

Main category: cs.CV, eess.SP

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出LM-SCIP框架，将LLM作为核心推理引擎，融合视觉与雷达多模态感知用于自动驾驶。通过通道自适应语义模块（CASM）动态调节雷达特征输入，结合LoRA微调的LLM与异构混合专家（H-MoE）进行语义推理，在nuScenes和VIRAT数据集上显著提升了定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-雷达融合对鲁棒自动驾驶至关重要，但实际中由于遮挡、恶劣天气和信道噪声，输入质量动态变化，传统静态数据融合方法难以应对。本文旨在将问题从静态数据融合重新定义为信道感知语义推理，以提升自动驾驶感知的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出LM-SCIP框架：1）分层雷达-视觉编码器提取多模态特征；2）通道自适应语义模块（CASM）将链路指标映射为"通道提示"，动态门控外部雷达特征；3）LoRA微调的LLM结合异构混合专家（H-MoE）仲裁局部视觉线索与信道条件化的雷达上下文；4）解耦多任务解码器输出定位、轨迹预测和图像重建。

Result: 在nuScenes上，相比纯视觉基线，LM-SCIP在雷达输入可控切换下将定位RMSE降低40.0%。在VIRAT上，定位RMSE为0.214m，minFDE(k=1)为0.179m。结果表明，低信噪比时模型退化为视觉主导的鲁棒方案，高信噪比时实现协同融合。

Conclusion: LM-SCIP通过LLM驱动的信道感知语义融合，有效解决了自动驾驶中视觉-雷达融合的动态质量变化问题，实现了鲁棒的多模态感知。该框架为将LLM作为推理核心用于自动驾驶感知提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文将LLM作为核心推理引擎融合视觉与雷达多模态感知，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉语言模型与MLLM范畴。虽然应用场景为自动驾驶，但核心方法是LLM驱动的多模态融合，与vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与MLLM）这一规范主题合理匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving radar-vision fusion, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: Autonomous driving, multimodal fusion, LLM reasoning, channel adaptation, radar-vision

Abstract: Vision-radar fusion is central to robust autonomous driving, combining dense visual semantics with precise range and velocity measurements from radar. However, real-world fusion quality is fundamentally challenged by dynamically varying input quality, stemming from occlusion, adverse weather, and channel noise. To address this, we re-frame the problem from static data fusion to channel-aware semantic reasoning and propose a Large Language Model-centric Semantic-layer Channel-aware Integrated Perception (LM-SCIP) framework. It places a Large Language Model (LLM) as a central reasoning core to fuse a local visual stream with a quality-varying external radar stream used to cover perception-blind spots. Concretely, LM-SCIP couples a hierarchical radar-vision encoder with a Channel-Adaptive Semantic Module (CASM) that maps link indicators into a "Channel Prompt" to dynamically gate external radar features. A parameter-efficient, LoRA-tuned LLM, in conjunction with a heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts (H-MoE), then arbitrates between local visual cues and the channel-conditioned radar context. Finally, a decoupled multi-task decoder outputs localization, trajectory forecasting, and image reconstruction. Experiments on nuScenes and VIRAT validate our approach. On nuScenes, under a controlled toggle of radar input, LM-SCIP reduces localization RMSE by 40.0% versus a vision-only baseline. On VIRAT, the model attains a 0.214m localization RMSE and 0.179m minFDE (k=1). These results reveal that the proposed LM-SCIP enables a robust vision-dominant fallback at low SNR and synergistic fusion at high SNR.

</details>


### [98] [Towards Real-World Ultrasound Understanding: Large Vision-Language Models from Multi-Image Examinations with Long-Form Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01908)
*Bingcong Yan, Chunlei Li, Jingliang Hu, Yilei Shi, Xiao Xiang Zhu, Lichao Mou*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (69.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含 150 万次超声检查、1770 万张图像的大规模数据集，以检查级别对齐多张图像与临床报告，使用 LoRA 微调标准 LVLM，无需复杂架构即实现了超声理解任务上的 SOTA 性能，并提供了模型与数据规模的缩放分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声成像具有复杂性和变异性，现有 LVLM 在超声领域的应用有限。作者重新审视实现真实世界超声理解的关键因素，认为数据规模和临床忠实的数据对齐比复杂架构更重要。

Method: 构建大规模超声数据集（1.5M 检查、17.7M 图像、多器官覆盖），以检查级别组织数据，将多张图像与对应的非结构化临床报告对齐。使用标准 LVLM 架构，通过 LoRA 微调，无需任务特定修改。

Result: 该简单方法在多种超声理解任务上超越了先前使用更复杂流程的方法。同时提供了模型和数据缩放分析，揭示了规模在超声 LVLM 中的作用。

Conclusion: 数据规模和临床忠实的数据对齐是实现真实世界超声理解的关键，标准 LVLM 配合大规模高质量数据即可取得优异性能，无需复杂架构设计。

Classification reason: 该论文构建大规模超声检查数据集，使用 LoRA 微调标准 LVLM 实现超声图像理解，属于通用视觉语言模型在医学领域的应用，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Solid medical domain application but lacks novelty in architecture or core methodology, and falls outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: Medical ultrasound, LVLM fine-tuning, LoRA, large-scale dataset, scaling analysis

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved strong performance across many medical imaging tasks, yet their application to ultrasound remains limited due to its inherent complexity and variability. In this work, we revisit what is truly needed to enable real-world ultrasound understanding. Instead of introducing complex architectures or elaborate training strategies, we show that data scale and clinically faithful data alignment are the key factors. We construct a large-scale dataset of 1.5M real-world ultrasound examinations, containing 17.7M images, multi-organ coverage, and paired uncurated clinical reports. Crucially, we organize the data at the examination level, aligning multiple images with their corresponding reports to reflect real clinical workflows. We then fine-tune a standard LVLM using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) on this dataset without task-specific modifications. Surprisingly, this simple recipe already leads to strong performance across diverse ultrasound understanding tasks, outperforming prior methods designed with more complex pipelines. Beyond these results, we present model and data scaling analyses that provide insights into the role of scale in ultrasound LVLMs.

</details>


### [99] [MolSight: A Graph-Aware Vision-Language Model for Unified Chemical Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01982)
*Wenda Wang, Yihan Tong, Yuwei Hu, Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, q-bio.BM

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (58.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MolSight 是一个图感知的视觉语言模型框架，通过分子拓扑模块注入化学键邻接信息，以及分子定位模块对齐视觉特征与化学符号语义，显著提升了 VLM 对分子图像的结构化理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子大语言模型和视觉语言模型难以充分捕捉分子结构的视觉表征，缺乏拓扑建模能力，导致对分子图像的结构理解不准确。

Method: MolSight 包含两个核心模块：(1) 分子拓扑模块（Molecular Topology Module），将化学键邻接信息注入视觉 token；(2) 分子定位模块（Molecular Grounding Module），对齐视觉特征与化学符号语义。

Result: 在多个化学视觉理解任务上，MolSight 显著优于现有 VLM、分子 LLM 和专用工具，达到了分子图像推理的新水平。

Conclusion: MolSight 证明了将图感知的拓扑建模与视觉语言模型结合，可以有效提升分子图像的结构化理解能力，为分子设计与药物发现提供了统一框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MolSight，一个面向分子图像理解的图感知视觉语言模型（VLM），属于通用视觉语言模型架构与多模态理解方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on molecular image understanding, which is outside your priority subtopics and matches negative keywords. Signals: molecular VLM, graph-aware, chemical image, drug discovery, negative keyword match

Abstract: Using molecular large language models (LLMs) as a unified framework for understanding molecular structures and functions is emerging as a new trend in tasks such as molecular design and drug discovery. However, these models struggle to fully capture the visual representation of molecular structures, limiting their potential. While existing molecular vision-language models (VLMs) show promise, they still face challenges in structural alignment and lack the necessary topological modeling for accurate molecular understanding. To address this, we propose MolSight, a graph-aware vision-language model framework designed to enhance the understanding of molecular images by VLMs. MolSight integrates a Molecular Topology Module to inject chemical-bond adjacency information into vision tokens, and a Molecular Grounding Module to align visual features with chemical symbolic semantics. Our experiments demonstrate that MolSight significantly outperforms existing VLMs, molecular LLMs, and specialized tools across multiple chemical visual understanding tasks, achieving a new level of molecular image reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [Show Me Examples: Inferring Visual Concepts from Image Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02402)
*Nick Stracke, Kolja Bauer, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Miguel Angel Bautista, Josh Susskind, Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (77.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VICIS 任务，评估 VLM 从图像集合中推断共享视觉概念并应用到新查询图像的能力。实验表明现有 VLM 在此任务上表现不佳，作者提出训练框架和架构，使模型能从图像集合中学习视觉概念并从查询中提取概念特定嵌入，在合成数据和 ImageNet/WordNet 上生成更准确多样的结果，并泛化到未见概念和草图模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 能理解复杂文本指令，但无法从纯视觉上下文中推理——即从少量示例图像中推断共享概念并应用到新输入。这种能力对于视觉概念学习、少样本泛化等场景至关重要，但目前缺乏相关任务定义和解决方案。

Method: 提出 VICIS 任务：给定一组共享概念的上下文图像和一张查询图像，模型需生成保留上下文概念且与查询一致的新图像。设计训练框架，包含概念编码器从图像集合中提取共享概念嵌入，以及查询编码器从查询图像中提取概念特定嵌入，两者结合后输入生成模型。

Result: 在合成数据和 ImageNet/WordNet 大规模数据上，所提模型生成结果比现有 VLM 更准确、更多样，并能泛化到未见概念和草图等不同模态。

Conclusion: VLM 在纯视觉概念推断任务上存在明显不足，本文提出的 VICIS 任务和训练框架有效弥补了这一差距，为视觉概念学习和少样本视觉推理提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 从图像集合中推断视觉概念的能力，属于视觉语言模型能力分析与改进，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses visual concept inference from sets, a novel VLM capability, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VICIS task, visual concept inference, VLM reasoning from image sets, concept-specific embeddings, generalization to sketches

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can follow complex textual instructions, yet they struggle to reason from purely visual context. In particular, current models fail to infer shared concepts from sets of example images and apply them to new inputs. We introduce Visual Concept Inference from Sets (VICIS), a task that evaluates this capability. Given a small context set of images sharing a concept and a query image, the model must generate new images that preserve the context-defined concept while remaining consistent with the query. We show that state-of-the-art VLMs perform poorly on this task, often ignoring the visual context or defaulting to biased generations. To address this gap, we propose a training framework and architecture that learn to infer visual concepts from image sets and extract concept-specific embeddings from queries. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale ImageNet/WordNet data show that our model generates more accurate and diverse outputs and generalizes to unseen concepts and modalities such as sketches.

</details>

### [101] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li, Lingxi Xie, Xinyue Huo, Jihao Qiu, Jiacheng Shao, Pengfei Chen, Jiannan Ge, Kaiwen Duan, Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出DramaSR-532K大规模说话人识别基准（53.2万标注对话行、900+角色）和DramaSR-LRM方法，利用大型推理模型（LRM）通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据，在长剧集视频中实现高保真说话人归因，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长剧集视频理解中，说话人识别（将每句语音归因到对应角色）是理解复杂剧情的关键，但现有方法在短语音片段上因声学生物特征不可靠而表现不佳，且缺乏大规模标注基准。

Method: (1) 构建DramaSR-532K基准，包含532K标注对话行、900+独特角色，融合听觉、语言和视觉线索。(2) 提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大型推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具使用（tool-use）自主聚合上下文证据（如面部识别、场景上下文、对话历史等），实现高保真归因。

Result: DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在短语音片段上表现突出，弥补了声学生物特征不可靠时的识别短板。

Conclusion: 大型推理模型结合多模态工具使用能有效提升长剧集视频中的说话人识别性能，DramaSR-532K为后续研究提供了大规模基准。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长剧集视频中说话人识别（Speaker Recognition），本质上是将每句语音归因到对应角色，需要融合听觉、语言和视觉线索进行多模态推理。这属于视频理解中的细粒度问答/归因任务，与多模态理解与生成方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题高度相关，因为该子主题涵盖VQA及细粒度视频理解任务。论文提出的DramaSR-532K基准和DramaSR-LRM方法均围绕多模态线索融合与推理展开。

Importance reason: Speaker recognition in TV dramas is a niche application with limited transfer to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: multimodal understanding, speaker recognition, long-form video, large reasoning model, benchmark contribution

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: .}

</details>


### [102] [A Cost-Aware, Paired Protocol for Auditing Dynamic Tool Synthesis in Agentic Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01469)
*Aseel Mohamed, Rama AlHamidi, Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi, Rasul Khanbayov, Erchin Serpedin, Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种成本感知的配对审计协议，用于评估工具增强型视频问答代理系统。该协议将两个系统在相同输入上配对，联合报告准确率和成本差异，将结果分为六类。作者在 Dynamic-SAGE 框架上实例化该协议，发现其相比基线准确率提升 7.5 分，推理轮次减少约 28%，但 token 使用量增加 34%、成本上升 26%，揭示了标量准确率指标无法反映的多维性能画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agentic VideoQA 系统的工具库是固定的，重复过程需从原语重建，造成推理开销。而标准评估仅关注最终答案准确率，忽略了推理成本，无法揭示系统如何在准确率和成本之间权衡。因此需要一种能联合评估准确率和成本的审计方法。

Method: 提出一种成本感知的配对审计协议：对每个问题，将两个系统在相同输入上配对，记录准确率和可见工具调用变化，将配对结果分为六类（如准确率提升且成本降低、准确率提升但成本增加等），使用 McNemar 检验和配对 bootstrap 置信区间报告显著性。实例化该协议于 Dynamic-SAGE 框架——该框架能合成、验证并持久注册可复用的复合工具，并在 SAGE-Bench 上与 SAGE 基线对比评估。

Result: Dynamic-SAGE 相比 SAGE 基线准确率提升 7.5 分（p<0.001），推理轮次和可见工具调用减少约 28%，但 token 使用量增加 34%、成本上升 26%。增益在视觉类和开放性问题中最大，在语言类和多模态问题上中性。残留失败集中在困难的开放性问题。

Conclusion: 标量准确率指标无法反映系统在准确率和成本之间的复杂权衡，所提出的配对审计协议能揭示多维性能画像，为工具增强型视频问答系统的评估提供了更全面的方法论。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Agentic VideoQA 系统，属于视频问答（VideoQA）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态理解与生成方向下的 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on auditing protocol for agentic VideoQA, not on core priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: cost-aware auditing protocol, agentic video QA, tool synthesis evaluation, accuracy-cost tradeoff, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Agentic Video Question Answering (VideoQA) systems invoke tools during inference, but their tool libraries are fixed, so recurring procedures are rebuilt from primitives on every question. Synthesizing composite tools could remove this overhead, but whether such expansion helps is hard to assess: final-answer accuracy, the standard metric, ignores inference effort, so it cannot reveal how a system shifts cost. We propose a cost-aware, paired protocol for auditing tool-augmented video agents. The protocol pairs two complete systems on the same input for each question and reports their net difference across accuracy and cost jointly. For each question, it sorts the paired outcome into one of six groups defined by joint correctness and by the change in visible tool calls, separating accuracy-preserving efficiency gains from harmful regressions. Significance is reported with McNemar's test and paired bootstrap confidence intervals. We instantiate the protocol on Dynamic-SAGE, an agentic VideoQA framework that synthesizes, validates, and persistently registers executable composite tools for reuse on unseen questions, and evaluate it against the SAGE baseline on SAGE-Bench. The audit reveals a multi-axis profile that a scalar accuracy comparison would miss: Dynamic-SAGE improves accuracy by 7.5 points (p < 0.001) and reduces reasoning turns and visible tool calls by roughly 28%, while shifting rather than reducing inference cost, as token usage rises 34% and cost 26%. Gains are largest on visual and open-ended questions and neutral on verbal and multimodal ones, and residual failures concentrate on hard, open-ended questions where the pipeline does the most work. By measuring accuracy and cost jointly, the protocol shows where the pipeline-level difference is reliable and where it is not. The code is available at .

</details>


### [103] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart, Chansong Lim, Nícolas Roque dos Santos, Yue Dong, Sheldon Peterson, Jia Chen, Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (45.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多模态铁路道口安全分析系统，利用铁路道口图像和结构化事故报告数据，通过微调紧凑型VLM进行安全评分与风险等级分类，与专家意见和FRA官方安全评分对齐，宏F1达0.757。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路道口安全评估对公共安全至关重要，但现有方法依赖人工检查或单一数据源，缺乏利用多模态数据（视觉+结构化报告）进行自动化、可扩展的安全评估系统。

Method: 提出一个多模态管道：1) 数据准备：收集铁路道口图像与FRA事故报告；2) 使用紧凑型VLM（视觉语言模型）进行微调，通过路由机制融合图像特征与结构化数据特征；3) 输出安全评分（与FRA评分对齐）和风险等级分类（HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK）。

Result: 系统在风险等级分类上达到宏F1=0.757，安全评分估计的RMSE=0.078，相关性=0.492，定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 多模态数据融合（视觉+结构化报告）能有效提升铁路道口安全评估的准确性，紧凑型VLM管道在资源受限场景下具有实用潜力，为基础设施安全评估提供了可扩展的AI方案。

Classification reason: 该论文利用多模态数据（铁路道口图像+结构化事故报告）进行安全评估与评分，本质上是一个视觉问答与多模态推理任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度相关。

Importance reason: This paper is a domain-specific safety application with no connection to the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: domain-specific safety analysis, no continuous language model, no video generation, no world model, no autoregressive generation

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [104] [Temporal and Cross-Modal Alignment for Enhanced Audiovisual Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01667)
*Chen Zhao, Jiajun Ma, Qilong Huang, Tiehan Fan, Hongyu Li, Zhuoliang Kang, Xiaoming Wei, Jian Yang, Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TCA-Captioner框架，通过Observer-Checker-Corrector迭代精炼策略生成高质量训练数据，并设计TCA-Bench基准来解耦评估视听视频字幕生成中的时序与跨模态对齐能力，在视听绑定和时序关系推理上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视听视频字幕生成中面临模态分离（modality detachment）和时序不连贯问题，难以准确地将听觉事件绑定到视觉实体或捕捉复杂的因果动态。

Method: 提出TCA-Captioner框架：(1) Observer-Checker-Corrector (OCC) 迭代精炼策略，生成高保真、细粒度的训练数据；(2) 利用高密度人机交互数据集优化模型以建模复杂的视听交互；(3) 提出TCA-Bench诊断基准，采用解耦评估协议（Decoupled Evaluation Protocol）分别量化视听绑定和时序关系推理能力。

Result: 大量实验表明TCA-Captioner在时序连贯和同步的视听叙事生成上达到了新标准，在视听绑定和时序关系推理方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过OCC迭代精炼策略和解耦评估协议，有效提升了视听视频字幕生成中的时序与跨模态对齐能力，为多模态视频理解提供了新的框架和评估范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视听视频字幕生成（audiovisual video captioning），属于视频理解与问答的范畴。虽然涉及音频-视觉跨模态对齐，但核心任务是生成视频描述文本，与多模态理解中的VQA/视频描述任务高度相关。映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc），该子主题涵盖VQA及视频描述类工作。

Importance reason: Focuses on audiovisual captioning alignment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: multimodal understanding, temporal alignment, cross-modal alignment, video captioning, benchmark

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced video understanding, achieving precise temporal and cross-modal alignment in audiovisual video captioning remains a formidable challenge. Most existing approaches suffer from modality detachment and temporal incoherence, failing to accurately bind auditory events to visual entities or capture complex causal dynamics. To address these deficiencies, we propose TCA-Captioner, a framework specifically engineered to enhance Temporal and Cross-Modal Alignment for audiovisual video captioning. We first introduce the Observer-Checker-Corrector (OCC) framework, an iterative refinement strategy that generates high-fidelity, meticulously grounded training data. Leveraging a curated high-density human interaction dataset, TCA-Captioner is optimized to model sophisticated audiovisual interactions. Furthermore, we present TCA-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark utilizing a Decoupled Evaluation Protocol to isolate and quantify model proficiency in audiovisual binding and temporal relational reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TCA-Captioner sets a new standard for temporally-coherent and synchronized audiovisual narratives.

</details>


### [105] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde, Halil Ibrahim Gulluk, Wim Van Criekinge, Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文将混合专家扩散语言模型 DiffusionGemma-26B 适配到医学视觉问答任务上，与同尺寸自回归模型 Gemma-4-26B 进行对比。在医学 VQA 数据集上，扩散模型达到或超过自回归模型性能，解码速度快 3.5-4.4 倍，并且具备自回归模型所缺乏的任意顺序填充（infill）能力，特别适合放射科报告草稿的交互式生成场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型几乎全部采用自回归架构，但扩散语言模型在双向去噪生成方面具有独特优势（如任意顺序填充），适合放射科报告这种需要交互式编辑和片段填充的实际场景。作者希望验证扩散语言模型在医学 VQA 上的能力，并利用其天然填充能力提升放射科报告起草效率。

Method: 采用 DiffusionGemma-26B（基于 Gemma-2 的混合专家扩散语言模型），通过 LoRA 微调适配医学 VQA 数据集。与同尺寸自回归模型 Gemma-4-26B 在相同 LoRA 设置下进行对比。使用对冗长度鲁棒的 LLM 评判器进行评分。利用扩散模型的双向去噪特性实现任意顺序填充（infill），即给定报告片段后填充中间缺失文本。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学 VQA 数据集上达到或超过自回归模型的性能；微调后的模型（3.8B 活跃参数）与前沿视觉语言模型具有竞争力；解码速度比自回归模型快 3.5-4.4 倍；扩散模型天然支持任意顺序填充，而自回归模型在此任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学 VQA 上可以匹敌甚至超越自回归模型，同时具备更快的解码速度和独特的任意顺序填充能力，特别适合放射科报告起草等需要交互式编辑的医学应用场景。

Classification reason: 论文将扩散语言模型应用于医学视觉问答（VQA）数据集，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一规范子主题。虽然也涉及扩散语言模型，但核心应用场景是医学VQA，因此映射到此规范子主题最为贴切。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VQA and radiology report drafting, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: Diffusion language model for medical VQA, 3.5-4.4x faster decoding than AR, any-order infill capability, matches AR performance, not aligned with user's continuous multimodal or video generation priorities

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [106] [Spatio-Temporal and Clinical Conditioning for Fine-Grained Radiology Report Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02024)
*P. Sloan, E. Simpson, M. Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 STAR3，一个多模态时空注意力检索框架，通过目标检测器识别解剖区域，结合当前临床上下文和前后影像变化进行条件化报告句子检索，在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上优于现有检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的放射学报告生成方法缺乏明确的解剖学定位，未考虑疾病纵向进展和可用临床上下文，无法反映真实临床报告实践。

Method: STAR3 框架使用目标检测器识别胸部X光片中的解剖区域，将区域级解剖信息与临床指征及前后影像变化对齐，通过时空注意力机制检索语义相关的报告句子，实现解剖学和时间上可溯源的报告生成。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上，STAR3 在检索指标、NLP 指标和临床指标上均优于现有基于检索的方法。

Conclusion: 解剖、时间和临床条件化检索对于推进自动化放射学报告生成具有重要价值，STAR3 框架有效提升了报告生成的临床相关性和准确性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注放射学报告检索与生成，属于医学视觉问答与文档理解范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_vqa_doc）中的医学VQA/文档理解方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on radiology report retrieval, which is outside your priority subtopics and negative-keyword domains. Signals: radiology report retrieval, anatomical grounding, longitudinal conditioning, MIMIC-CXR, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Radiology is vital to modern healthcare, but rising imaging demand and persistent workforce shortages strain reporting capacity and clinical workflows. Automated radiology report generation has the potential to support radiologists and help alleviate this burden; however, existing retrieval-based methods remain rigid, lack explicit anatomical grounding, and do not account for longitudinal disease progression or available clinical context. In this work, we introduce STAR3, a multimodal, spatio-temporal, attentive retrieval framework for radiology report generation that aligns region-level anatomical information with clinical indications and longitudinal changes across chest X-ray studies. Our framework employs an object detector to identify anatomically meaningful regions and retrieves semantically relevant report sentences conditioned on both current clinical context and changes observed between prior and current examinations. This design enables anatomically and temporally grounded report generation that better reflects clinical reporting practice. Experiments on the MIMIC-CXR dataset demonstrate that STAR3 outperforms current retrieval-based approaches on retrieval, NLP and clinical metrics, highlighting the value of conditioning retrieval anatomically, temporally and clinically for advancing automated radiology report generation.

</details>


### [107] [Evaluating Vision-Language Models as a Zero-Shot Learning Alternative to You Only Look Once and Optical Character Recognition for Nigerian License Plate Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02025)
*Ismail Ismail Tijjani, Ahmad Abubakar Mustapaha, Sunusi Ibrahim Muhammad, Muhammad Bashir Aliyu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (46.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文探索了视觉语言模型（VLM）作为尼日利亚车牌识别的零样本学习方案，评估了5种VLM在88张真实图像上的表现，发现Gemini和Qwen在字符错误率上显著优于其他模型，并展示了VLM相比传统YOLO+OCR管线的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统车牌识别系统依赖YOLO检测+OCR的多阶段管线，存在资源需求高、非结构化环境性能差、需要大量标注数据等局限。本文探索VLM作为统一零样本解决方案的可行性，特别是在尼日利亚等发展中国家非标准车牌场景下的应用。

Method: 收集88张尼日利亚真实场景车牌图像，评估5种VLM（Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp、Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct、GPT-4o、Claude 4 Sonnet、Llama 3.2 Vision 90b）的零样本车牌识别能力，以字符错误率（CER）为评价指标。

Result: Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp和Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct在字符错误率上显著优于其他模型，在挑战性场景下展现出更高的准确性和鲁棒性。VLM作为统一零样本方案相比传统YOLO+OCR管线具有实际优势。

Conclusion: VLM可以作为传统YOLO+OCR车牌识别管线的有效零样本替代方案，但不同VLM之间性能差异显著，模型提供商的性能声明需要谨慎对待。

Classification reason: 该论文评估VLM在车牌识别（LPR）任务上的零样本能力，属于视觉问答与文档理解（VQA/OCR相关）范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"也有一定关联，但核心任务是OCR/文档理解，因此映射到multimodal_vqa_doc。

Importance reason: This paper is a narrow application study on Nigerian license plate recognition, far from the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: License plate recognition, zero-shot VLM evaluation, YOLO+OCR comparison, small dataset (88 images), no continuous token or generation focus

Abstract: License Plate Recognition (LPR) systems are critical tools in traffic monitoring, security enforcement, and urban mobility management. Traditional LPR systems often rely on a multi-stage pipeline involving object detection using You Only Look Once (YOLO) and Optical Character Recognition (OCR), which suffer from limitations such as high resource demands, poor performance in unstructured environments, and the need for large annotated datasets. This study explores the potential of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified, zeroshot learning solution for Nigerian license plate recognition. Using a curated dataset of 88 challenging real-world images collected in Nigeria, we evaluate five selected VLMs: Gemini 2.0 Flash Exp (Google DeepMind), Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct (Alibaba), GPT-4o (OpenAI), Claude 4 Sonnet (Anthropic), and Llama 3.2 Vision 90b (Meta). Results based on Character Error Rate (CER) reveal that Gemini and Qwen significantly outperform other models in both accuracy and robustness, on the challenging image scenarios. This work highlights the practical advantages of VLMs over YOLO+OCR, questions the claims by model providers, and compares the performances of the VLMs.

</details>

### [108] [ReQuest: Rethinking-based Question-Aware Frame Selection for Long-Form Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01737)
*Minkuk Kim, Suyong Yun, Young Tae Kim, Jinyoung Moon, Jinwoo Choi, Seong Tae Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReQuest 提出了一种基于不确定性的、问题自适应的关键帧选择流水线，用于长视频问答。它通过轻量级问题感知选择器、重思考路由和不确定性引导的自适应非极大值抑制，在不修改底层 MLLM 的情况下提升长视频 QA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频问答在固定输入 token 预算下，均匀采样难以有效定位证据帧，导致模型无法准确回答问题。现有方法缺乏对问题意图与视频内容之间对齐的显式建模。

Method: ReQuest 包含三个组件：(i) 轻量级问题感知选择器，通过 MLLM 生成的监督信号蒸馏训练；(ii) 重思考路由（Re-thinking Routing），仅在模型不确定时根据长度自适应标准触发额外推理；(iii) 不确定性引导的自适应非极大值抑制，选择时序上多样化的帧并根据问题难度调整帧间距。

Result: 在 Video-MME、MLVU 和 LongVideoBench 上，ReQuest 在中等和长视频场景下均取得一致的准确率提升，且计算开销具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ReQuest 作为一种即插即用方法，有效提升了长视频 QA 性能，无需微调底层 MLLM，验证了问题自适应关键帧选择在长视频理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究长视频问答中的关键帧选择问题，属于视频理解与时序推理（video_understanding_temporal）这一[CANONICAL]子主题，具体涉及长视频理解与视频问答。

Importance reason: Focuses on video QA frame selection, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Long-form video QA, Question-adaptive frame selection, Uncertainty-driven, Plug-and-play MLLM, Video-MME, MLVU, LongVideoBench

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have substantially advanced video understanding, yet long-form video QA remains challenging under fixed input token budgets, where uniform sampling can be inefficient for evidence localization. We propose ReQuest , an uncertainty-driven, question-adaptive keyframe selection pipeline that aligns question intent with relevant video content through selective computation. ReQuest integrates (i) a lightweight question-aware selector distilled from MLLM-generated supervision, (ii) Re-thinking Routing that triggers additional inference only when the model is uncertain with a length-adaptive criterion, and (iii) uncertainty-guided adaptive non-maximum suppression that selects temporally diverse frames while adjusting spacing based on question difficulty. As a plug-andplay method, ReQuest improves long-video QA without modifying or fine-tuning the underlying MLLM. Experiments on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench demonstrate consistent accuracy gains with competitive computational cost, with particularly strong improvements in medium and long video regimes.

</details>


### [109] [Learning to Evolve Scenes: Reasoning about Human Activities with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02425)
*Francesca Pistilli, Simone Alberto Peirone, Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SG-Ego 数据集（基于 Ego4D 的时空场景图标注）和 GLEN 图模型，用于第一视角视频中人类活动驱动的场景演化推理，并定义了活动驱动的图编辑预测（A-GEF）新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式视觉或语言对齐表示，缺乏对场景动态的结构化推理。作者认为显式、可组合、可编辑的人-环境交互表示对于丰富的活动理解至关重要。

Method: 构建 SG-Ego 数据集，将 Ego4D 视频扩展为时空场景图（随时间演化的关系三元组）。提出 GLEN 模型，基于图神经网络对场景图序列进行编码，同时对齐文本动作描述并建模时序演化。定义 A-GEF 任务，将场景动态建模为动作条件驱动的结构化变换序列。

Result: 在 EgoMCQ、EgoCVR、EXPLORE-Bench 和 A-GEF 等多个基准上，GLEN 相比原始视频基线取得强结果，在推理场景中表现优异，通常只有 MLLM 才能处理的推理任务也能胜任。

Conclusion: 时空场景图结合图推理模型可作为视频理解中强有力且可解释的组合式表示，有望推广到更广泛的场景。

Classification reason: 论文关注第一视角视频中人类活动的场景图时序推理，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 canonical subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Scene graph reasoning for video understanding is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct alignment with user's focus on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: scene graph, video understanding, human activity reasoning, Ego4D dataset, graph edit forecasting

Abstract: Understanding human behavior while interacting with the surrounding world is crucial for many applications of embodied AI. First-person videos are particularly informative for this problem, as they well capture how activities reshape the scene over time. However, existing approaches often rely on implicit visual or language-aligned representations, disregarding structured reasoning over the scene dynamic. We argue that explicit, compositional and editable representations of human-environment interactions can play a crucial role for rich grounded activity understanding. To this end, we introduce SG-Ego, a large scale annotation set extending Ego4D with spatio-temporal scene graphs, where relations triplets are consolidated over time into explicit time-evolving descriptions of the scene state. To reason over this representation, we propose GLEN, a graph-based model that operates over scene graph sequences to both align them with textual actions and model their temporal evolution. In addition, we formulate the activity-driven graph-edit forecasting (A-GEF) problem, a novel task that casts scene dynamics as a sequence of structured transformations conditioned on ongoing actions, enabling explicit reasoning about how scenes change over time. We validate our approach across multiple downstream tasks, spanning retrieval benchmarks as EgoMCQ and EgoCVR, as well as long-horizon reasoning benchmarks as EXPLORE-Bench and the newly introduced A-GEF. GLEN achieves strong results compared to raw video baselines and it excels in reasoning settings, typically addressed only with MLLMs, while enabling controllable and structured predictions of scene dynamics driven by human activities. We believe our results establish spatio-temporal scene graphs, together with models that reason over them, as strong compositional and interpretable representations for video understanding and potentially beyond.

</details>
